<tr><td>↳反 compassionate</td><td>/kəmˈpæʃənət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有同情心的，慈悲的</td><td>compassionate care<br>充满关怀的照护</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A compassionate nurse sat with the frightened child all night.  一位有同情心的护士整夜陪着那个受惊的孩子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 humane</td><td>/hjuˈmeɪn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>人道的，仁慈的</td><td>humane treatment of prisoners<br>人道地对待囚犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Activists demanded more humane conditions inside the detention center.  活动人士要求改善拘留中心内的人道条件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 kind</td><td>/ˈkaɪnd/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>仁慈的，友好的；种类</td><td>a kind word<br>一句善意的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was kind of her to visit the sick neighbor daily.  她每天去看望生病的邻居，真是好心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 kindhearted</td><td>/ˌkaɪndˈhɑrtɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>好心的，仁慈的</td><td>a kindhearted neighbor<br>好心的邻居</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A kindhearted stranger paid for the child's bus ticket.  一位好心的陌生人替那孩子付了车票。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 merciful</td><td>/ˈmɜːrsɪfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>仁慈的，宽厚的</td><td>a merciful judge<br>宽仁的法官</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court was merciful and gave him only a small fine.  法庭很宽仁，只罚了他少许款项。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sympathetic</td><td>/ˌsɪmpəˈθetɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>同情的，有同情心的</td><td>a sympathetic listener<br>富有同情心的倾听者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nurse gave her a sympathetic look and held her hand.  护士同情地看着她，握住了她的手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>vicissitude</b></del></td><td>/vɪˈsɪsɪtuːd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>自然变化，变迁兴衰</td><td>the vicissitudes of life<br>人生的兴衰变迁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her memoir traces the vicissitudes of one family across three centuries.  她的回忆录追述了一个家族三百年的兴衰变迁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fluctuation</td><td>/ˌflʌktʃuˈeɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>波动，起伏</td><td>price fluctuation<br>价格波动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sharp fluctuations in demand ruined the small supplier.  需求剧烈波动毁了这家小供应商。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mutation</td><td>/mjuˈteɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>变化，转变</td><td>a sudden mutation<br>突然的变化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The language shows a slow mutation over four centuries.  这门语言在四个世纪中缓慢变化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shift</td><td>/ˈʃɪft/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>转变，改变；移动</td><td>shift one's position<br>改变立场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Public opinion shifted sharply after the televised debate.  电视辩论后民意急剧转变。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 variation</td><td>/ˌveriˈeɪʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>变化，变体</td><td>slight variation in color<br>颜色的细微变化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is wide variation in rainfall across the region.  该地区降雨量差异很大。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 uniformity</td><td>/ˌjuːnəˈfɔːrməti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>一致，无变化</td><td>dull uniformity<br>单调的一致</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new houses had a depressing uniformity of design.  这些新房子设计一致得令人压抑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>victimize</b></td><td>/ˈvɪktɪmaɪz/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使受骗</td><td>victimize elderly buyers<br>欺骗老年买家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fraudulent callers often victimize elderly buyers who trust strangers easily.  诈骗电话常欺骗那些容易相信陌生人的老年买家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cheat</td><td>/ˈtʃiːt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>欺骗，作弊；骗子</td><td>cheat on an exam<br>考试作弊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He cheated the old couple out of their entire life savings.  他骗走了这对老夫妇一生的积蓄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cozen</td><td>/ˈkʌzn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>诱骗</td><td>cozen him into agreeing<br>诱骗他同意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The swindler cozened elderly widows out of their modest life savings.  那个骗子诱骗年老的寡妇们交出微薄的积蓄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deceive</td><td>/dɪˈsiːv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>欺骗，误导</td><td>deceive the public<br>欺骗公众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He deceived his investors with promises of quick and easy profits.  他用轻松快速获利的承诺欺骗了投资者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 defraud</td><td>/dɪˈfrɔːd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诈骗，骗取</td><td>defraud investors<br>诈骗投资者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He defrauded dozens of families of their savings.  他骗走了数十个家庭的积蓄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dupe</td><td>/duːp/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>欺骗</td><td>dupe trusting investors<br>欺骗轻信的投资者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scheme duped trusting investors out of nearly a million dollars.  这个骗局欺骗轻信的投资者，骗走近百万美元。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fool</td><td>/ˈfuːl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>傻瓜，蠢人；愚弄</td><td>make a fool of oneself<br>让自己出丑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He felt like a fool after believing the obvious lie.  信了那个明显的谎言后，他觉得自己像个傻瓜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hoax</td><td>/hoʊks/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>欺骗</td><td>an elaborate hoax<br>精心设计的骗局</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The photograph turned out to be an elaborate hoax created by students.  那张照片原来是学生们精心设计的骗局。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 swindle</td><td>/ˈswɪndl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>欺骗，骗取</td><td>swindle investors out of money<br>骗取投资者的钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The broker swindled elderly investors out of their life savings.  经纪人骗取老年投资者的毕生积蓄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disabuse</td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈbjuːz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>打消错误念头，纠正</td><td>disabuse him of the notion<br>打消他的错误念头</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took hours to disabuse him of the notion that the test was simple.  花了好几个小时才打消他认为考试很简单的错误念头。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 disenchant</del></td><td>/ˌdɪsɪnˈtʃænt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使清醒</td><td>disenchant the young voters<br>使年轻选民清醒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years in politics disenchanted her with grand promises.  多年从政使她对宏大承诺不再抱幻想。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disillusion</td><td>/ˌdɪsɪˈluːʒən/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使醒悟，使幻想破灭</td><td>disillusion his followers<br>让追随者幻想破灭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The leaked memo disillusioned even loyal employees.  泄露的备忘录让连忠诚的员工都幻想破灭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 undeceive</del></td><td>/ˌʌndɪˈsiːv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使醒悟，使不再受骗</td><td>undeceive the public<br>使公众醒悟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Someone must undeceive her before she invests everything in that scheme.  必须有人让她醒悟，别把全部钱投进那个计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>vigilant</b></td><td>/ˈvɪdʒɪlənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>警醒的，警惕的</td><td>remain vigilant<br>保持警惕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Guards must remain vigilant throughout the long and quiet night shift.  守卫在漫长安静的夜班中必须始终保持警惕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 alert</td><td>/əˈlɜːrt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>警觉的，机敏的；警报</td><td>stay alert<br>保持警觉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Night guards must stay alert even during the quietest hours.  夜间守卫即便在最安静的时段也必须保持警觉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 attentive</td><td>/əˈtentɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>留心的，专注的；周到的</td><td>an attentive audience<br>专注的听众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nurse stayed attentive to every change in his breathing.  护士留心他呼吸的每一点变化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 awake</td><td>/əˈweɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>醒着的；唤醒</td><td>lie awake at night<br>夜里醒着躺着</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The noise from the street kept me awake until dawn.  街上的噪音让我一直醒到黎明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cautious</td><td>/ˈkɔːʃəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>谨慎的，小心的</td><td>a cautious approach<br>谨慎的做法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Investors remained cautious until the election results were announced.  在选举结果公布前投资者一直很谨慎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 observant</td><td>/əbˈzɜːrvənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>警觉的；严格遵守规章的</td><td>an observant guard<br>警觉的守卫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An observant guard noticed the broken lock before the thieves could return.  一名警觉的守卫在窃贼返回前注意到了被弄坏的锁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sharp</td><td>/ˈʃɑːrp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>尖刻的；锋利的；敏锐的</td><td>a sharp tongue<br>说话尖刻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The critic has a sharp tongue but an unusually fair mind.  这位评论家言辞尖刻，心思却格外公正。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 watchful</td><td>/ˈwɑːtʃfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>警惕的，注意的</td><td>a watchful eye<br>警惕的目光</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children played under their grandmother's watchful eye.  孩子们在祖母警惕的注视下玩耍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 careless</td><td>/ˈkerləs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗心的，疏忽的</td><td>a careless mistake<br>粗心的错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One careless remark ruined months of patient negotiation.  一句不慎的话毁了数月的耐心谈判。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 heedless</td><td>/ˈhiːdləs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不注意的，不顾的</td><td>heedless of danger<br>不顾危险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He ran into the street heedless of the traffic.  他不顾车流冲进了街道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inattentive</td><td>/ˌɪnəˈtentɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不注意的，漫不经心的</td><td>an inattentive student<br>不专心的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The inattentive driver failed to see the red light.  这位漫不经心的司机没看到红灯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unmindful</td><td>/ʌnˈmaɪndfəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不留意的，未察觉的</td><td>unmindful of danger<br>不顾危险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Unmindful of the time, they talked until well past midnight.  他们没在意时间，一直聊到深夜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unthinking</td><td>/ʌnˈθɪŋkɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不体贴的，欠考虑的</td><td>an unthinking remark<br>欠考虑的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His unthinking comment hurt her more than he realized.  他那句欠考虑的话伤她比他以为的更深。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unwary</td><td>/ʌnˈweri/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不警惕的，粗心的</td><td>trap the unwary traveler<br>诱骗粗心的旅客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Such websites are designed to trick the unwary buyer.  这类网站专门欺骗不加防范的买家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>vignette</b></td><td>/vɪnˈjet/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>简介，短文</td><td>a vignette of childhood<br>童年生活小品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The memoir opens with a charming vignette of childhood in Paris.  回忆录以一段迷人的巴黎童年小品文开篇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 definition</td><td>/ˌdefəˈnɪʃən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>描述，界定</td><td>a clear definition<br>清晰的界定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The essay offers a vivid definition of small-town boredom.  这篇文章生动地界定了小镇的无聊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 delineation</td><td>/dɪˌlɪniˈeɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>描绘，刻画</td><td>careful delineation of character<br>细致的人物刻画</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her delineation of the old teacher is unforgettable.  她对那位老教师的刻画令人难忘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 depiction</td><td>/dɪˈpɪkʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>描写，描绘</td><td>a vivid depiction<br>生动的描写</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel's depiction of factory work feels entirely true.  小说对工厂劳作的描写十分真实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 picture</td><td>/ˈpɪktʃər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>想象，设想；图画</td><td>picture himself as a hero<br>想象自己是英雄</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Try to picture the room as it looked a century ago.  试着想象这房间一百年前的样子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 portrait</td><td>/ˈpɔːrtrət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>描写，写照；肖像</td><td>a portrait of the artist<br>艺术家的写照</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The book is a warm portrait of his grandmother.  这本书是对他祖母的温情写照。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 portraiture</td><td>/ˈpɔrtrətʃər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>描绘，人物写照</td><td>literary portraiture<br>文学人物描写</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His portraiture of country priests filled three volumes.  他对乡村神父的描写写满了三卷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 portrayal</td><td>/pɔrˈtreɪəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>描绘，刻画</td><td>a sympathetic portrayal<br>富有同情的刻画</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her portrayal of the widow won a national prize.  她对那位寡妇的刻画获得了全国奖项。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rendering</td><td>/ˈrendərɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>描述，表现</td><td>a faithful rendering<br>忠实的表现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film's rendering of the trial was surprisingly exact.  影片对审判的表现出人意料地准确。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sketch</td><td>/ˈsketʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>小品，素描；简述</td><td>a character sketch<br>人物小品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wrote a short sketch of each neighbor on the street.  他为街上每位邻居写了一篇小品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>vigorous</b></td><td>/ˈvɪɡərəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>精力旺盛的，强有力的</td><td>vigorous exercise<br>剧烈运动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors recommend thirty minutes of vigorous exercise daily.  医生建议每天进行三十分钟的高强度运动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 brisk</td><td>/brɪsk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满生机的，有活力的</td><td>a brisk walk<br>快步散步</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A brisk walk before breakfast leaves me alert all morning.  早餐前快走一趟能让我整个上午都很清醒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dynamic</td><td>/daɪˈnæmɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>有活力的，动态的</td><td>a dynamic leader<br>有活力的领导者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city has a dynamic arts scene that keeps changing.  这座城市有着不断变化的活跃艺术圈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 energetic</td><td>/ˌenərˈdʒetɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>精力充沛的</td><td>an energetic performance<br>活力四射的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Even at seventy she remains remarkably energetic and full of plans.  即使七十岁了，她依然精力充沛，计划满满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lively</td><td>/ˈlaɪvli/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>活跃的，生气勃勃的</td><td>a lively debate<br>热烈的辩论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A lively debate broke out over the proposed budget.  围绕拟定预算爆发了一场热烈的辩论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 robust</td><td>/roʊˈbʌst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>精力充沛的；强壮的，健康的</td><td>robust good health<br>强健的好身体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At eighty he remains robust enough to work in the garden daily.  他八十岁了，身体依然强健，每天还能在园中劳作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spirited</td><td>/ˈspɪrɪtɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>生气勃勃的，活跃的</td><td>a spirited debate<br>一场热烈的辩论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The candidates held a spirited debate about tax policy.  候选人就税收政策展开了热烈的辩论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vital</td><td>/ˈvaɪtəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满活力的；极重要的</td><td>full of vital energy<br>充满生命活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Regular exercise keeps older people vital and mentally alert.  经常锻炼使老年人保持活力和头脑清醒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dull</td><td>/dʌl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>迟钝的；乏味的</td><td>a dull student<br>迟钝的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lecture was so dull that half the class slept.  讲座乏味得让一半学生睡着了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lethargic</td><td>/ləˈθɑːrdʒɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>没精打采的，行动迟缓的</td><td>feel lethargic all day<br>整天没精打采</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The heat left everyone lethargic and unwilling to move before evening.  酷热让所有人都没精打采，天黑前都不愿动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 listless</td><td>/ˈlɪstləs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无精打采的，倦怠的</td><td>feel listless and tired<br>感到倦怠疲乏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fever left her listless for nearly a week.  发烧让她将近一周都无精打采。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sluggish</td><td>/ˈslʌɡɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>行动迟缓的，懒散的</td><td>feel sluggish all morning<br>整个上午都懒洋洋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He felt sluggish all morning and could barely finish his simplest chores.  他整个上午都懒洋洋的，连最简单的杂事都几乎做不完。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 torpid</td><td>/ˈtɔːrpɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>迟钝的，无生气的</td><td>a torpid winter afternoon<br>慵懒迟滞的冬日下午</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The heat left the whole village torpid and unwilling to move.  酷热让整个村子都变得麻木迟钝，不愿动弹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>vilify</b></td><td>/ˈvɪlɪfaɪ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诽谤，辱骂</td><td>vilify the whistleblower<br>诽谤举报者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The newspaper tried to vilify the whistleblower instead of examining her evidence.  报纸试图诽谤举报者，而不是审查她的证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 asperse</del></td><td>/əˈspɜrs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诽谤，中伤</td><td>asperse his character<br>中伤他的品格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pamphlet aspersed every officer who supported the reform.  这本小册子中伤了每一位支持改革的官员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blacken</td><td>/ˈblækən/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>使变黑；诽谤，玷污</td><td>blacken his reputation<br>玷污他的名声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rumors quickly blackened the name of the honest official.  谣言很快玷污了这位清廉官员的名声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 calumniate</del></td><td>/kəˈlʌmnɪeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>诽谤，造谣，中伤</td><td>calumniate a rival<br>诽谤竞争对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Opponents calumniated the mayor with stories no one could prove.  反对者用无人能证实的说法诽谤市长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 defame</td><td>/dɪˈfeɪm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诽谤，中伤</td><td>defame a public figure<br>诽谤公众人物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He sued the paper for trying to defame him.  他起诉那家报纸企图诽谤他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 libel</td><td>/ˈlaɪbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>书面诽谤；（以文字）诽谤</td><td>sue for libel<br>以诽谤罪起诉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The actor claimed the article libeled him and his family.  这位演员称该文诽谤了他和他的家人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 malign</td><td>/məˈlaɪn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>恶意的；诽谤</td><td>malign his motives<br>诋毁他的动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rival newspapers began to malign the senator without offering a shred of evidence.  竞争对手的报纸开始诽谤这位参议员，却拿不出一丝证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 smear</td><td>/ˈsmɪr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>诽谤，抹黑；涂抹</td><td>a smear campaign<br>抹黑运动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rivals tried to smear the candidate with unfounded financial rumors.  对手试图用毫无根据的财务谣言抹黑这位候选人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 traduce</del></td><td>/trəˈduːs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诽谤，中伤</td><td>traduce an opponent<br>中伤对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rival papers traduced the senator throughout the long campaign.  对立的报纸在整场竞选中中伤这位参议员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 acclaim</td><td>/əˈkleɪm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>称赞</td><td>win critical acclaim<br>赢得评论界赞誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her first novel won wide critical acclaim across Europe and Asia.  她的第一部小说在欧洲和亚洲赢得了广泛的评论界赞誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 applaud</td><td>/əˈplɔːd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>称赞，赞成；鼓掌</td><td>applaud the decision<br>赞成这一决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Teachers applauded the decision to reduce homework for young pupils.  老师们赞成给低年级学生减少作业的决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 commend</td><td>/kəˈmend/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞扬，表扬</td><td>commend her courage<br>赞扬她的勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The principal commended her courage in front of the whole school.  校长在全校面前赞扬了她的勇气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 praise</td><td>/ˈpreɪz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>赞扬，称赞</td><td>win high praise<br>获得高度赞扬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her first novel won high praise from serious critics.  她的第一部小说赢得了严肃评论家的高度赞扬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>vim</b></td><td>/vɪm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>活力，精力</td><td>full of vim and vigor<br>精力充沛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At seventy he still worked with surprising vim and good humor.  七十岁的他干活仍精力惊人，心情愉快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 animation</td><td>/ˌænəˈmeɪʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>生气，活力</td><td>speak with animation<br>兴致勃勃地讲话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She described the trip with unusual animation and plenty of gestures.  她异常兴致勃勃地描述了那次旅行，还比划了许多手势。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bounce</td><td>/ˈbaʊns/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>驱逐，开除；弹跳</td><td>bounce him from the club<br>把他逐出俱乐部</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager bounced two waiters for stealing tips.  经理因偷小费开除了两名侍者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dynamism</td><td>/ˈdaɪnəˌmɪzəm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>活力，干劲</td><td>economic dynamism<br>经济活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city's dynamism attracts young workers every year.  这座城市的活力每年吸引年轻劳动者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 energy</td><td>/ˈenərdʒi/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>精力，活力；能量</td><td>full of restless energy<br>充满旺盛的精力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children still had plenty of energy after the long hike.  长途徒步之后孩子们仍精力充沛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 liveliness</td><td>/ˈlaɪvlinəs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>活力，生气</td><td>the liveliness of the market<br>市场的热闹活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The liveliness of the class surprised the visiting teacher.  班上的活力让来访的老师惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 robustness</td><td>/roʊˈbʌstnəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>强健，稳健</td><td>the robustness of the design<br>设计的稳健性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Engineers praised the robustness of the old bridge.  工程师赞赏这座旧桥的稳固。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 verve</td><td>/vɜːrv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>活力，热情</td><td>play with verve<br>充满激情地演奏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young pianist played that difficult passage with remarkable verve.  这位年轻钢琴家以非凡的热情演奏了那段高难度乐章。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vibrancy</td><td>/ˈvaɪbrənsi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>活力，生机</td><td>urban vibrancy<br>城市的活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New cafes restored some vibrancy to the old harbor.  新开的咖啡馆让老码头恢复了些活力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 vigorousness</del></td><td>/ˈvɪɡərəsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>精力充沛，强健</td><td>surprising vigorousness<br>出人意料的精力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At eighty he kept a vigorousness that shamed younger men.  八十岁的他仍精力充沛，让年轻人自愧不如。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vitality</td><td>/vaɪˈtæləti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>活力，生命力</td><td>brim with vitality<br>充满活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old market still brims with vitality on early summer mornings.  夏日清晨，这座老市场依然充满活力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lethargy</td><td>/ˈleθərdʒi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>无生气，倦怠，昏睡</td><td>shake off his lethargy<br>摆脱他的倦怠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A short walk helped him shake off his afternoon lethargy.  短短的散步帮他摆脱了午后的倦怠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 listlessness</td><td>/ˈlɪstləsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>倦怠，无精打采</td><td>an air of listlessness<br>一副无精打采的样子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A strange listlessness settled over the office in August.  八月里办公室弥漫着一种奇怪的倦怠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sluggishness</td><td>/ˈslʌɡɪʃnəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>迟缓，无精打采</td><td>morning sluggishness<br>早晨的迟缓无力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The heat explained the general sluggishness of the crew.  炎热解释了船员们普遍的无精打采。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 torpidity</del></td><td>/tɔːrˈpɪdəti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>迟钝，麻木</td><td>sink into torpidity<br>陷入麻木迟钝</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Winter torpidity kept the frogs buried in the mud.  冬日的麻木状态让青蛙埋在泥中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>vindicate</b></td><td>/ˈvɪndɪkeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>为…平反，为…辩护，使无罪；证明，证实</td><td>vindicate one's reputation<br>为自己的名誉洗冤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The results vindicated her unusual approach.  结果证明了她那不寻常的方法是对的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 absolve</td><td>/əbˈzɑːlv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>使无罪，解除责任</td><td>absolve someone of blame<br>免除某人的责任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report absolved the pilot of all blame.  报告免除了飞行员的全部责任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 acquit</td><td>/əˈkwɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>宣判无罪</td><td>acquit of murder<br>宣判谋杀罪不成立</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The jury acquitted him of all charges.  陪审团宣判他所有罪名不成立。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exculpate</td><td>/ˈekskʌlpeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>声明无罪；开脱，使无罪</td><td>exculpate the accused<br>为被告开脱罪责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New evidence exculpated the accused man after ten years in prison.  新证据在这名被告入狱十年后为他开脱了罪责。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exonerate</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɑːnəreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>免除责备</td><td>exonerate from blame<br>免除责备</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New evidence exonerated the wrongly jailed man.  新证据为这名被误判入狱者洗清了罪名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 attest</td><td>/əˈtest/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证实，为…作证</td><td>attest to his good character<br>证明他品行良好</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several neighbors will attest to his good character in court.  几位邻居将在法庭上证明他品行良好。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 authenticate</td><td>/ɔˈθentəˌkeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证实，鉴定为真</td><td>authenticate the painting<br>鉴定画作真伪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Experts were called in to authenticate the painting's signature.  专家被请来鉴定这幅画签名的真伪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 certify</td><td>/ˈsɜːrtəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证明，认证</td><td>certify the results as valid<br>证明结果有效</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An independent lab must certify that the water is safe.  必须由独立实验室证明水质安全。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 corroborate</td><td>/kəˈrɑːbəreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>用证据或权威证实；为…提供证据，支持</td><td>corroborate the witness's story<br>证实证人的说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two neighbors corroborated the driver's account of the accident that night.  两位邻居证实了司机对那晚事故的说法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 substantiate</td><td>/səbˈstænʃieɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证实</td><td>substantiate the claim<br>证实这一说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No evidence has emerged to substantiate his serious accusations.  没有证据能证实他严重的指控。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 support</td><td>/səˈpɔːrt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>支持，支撑</td><td>support a proposal<br>支持一项提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most members voted to support the new proposal.  大多数成员投票支持这项新提案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 validate</td><td>/ˈvælədeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证实，确认；使有效</td><td>validate the results<br>验证这些结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Independent labs validated the results within three months.  独立实验室在三个月内验证了这些结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 verify</td><td>/ˈverɪfaɪ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证实，核实</td><td>verify the data<br>核实这些数据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two independent labs will verify the results before publication.  两家独立实验室将在发表前核实这些结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 incriminate</td><td>/ɪnˈkrɪmɪneɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>归罪于</td><td>incriminate oneself<br>使自己受牵连</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He refused to answer questions that might incriminate him.  他拒绝回答可能使自己入罪的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disprove</td><td>/ˌdɪsˈpruːv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>反驳，证明为假</td><td>disprove the theory<br>驳倒这一理论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Magellan's voyage finally disproved the belief in a flat earth.  麦哲伦的航行最终驳倒了地球是平的这一信念。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rebut</td><td>/rɪˈbʌt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>反驳，驳斥</td><td>rebut the accusation<br>反驳这项指控</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She rebutted every charge with documents and dated photographs.  她用文件和标注日期的照片反驳了每项指控。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 refute</td><td>/rɪˈfjuːt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>驳倒，反驳</td><td>refute the claim<br>驳斥这一说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scientist used new data to refute the claim about rising temperatures.  这位科学家用新数据反驳了关于气温上升的说法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>vindictive</b></td><td>/vɪnˈdɪktɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>报复心强的；怀恨的</td><td>a vindictive campaign<br>报复性的行动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His vindictive lawsuit was clearly meant to punish a former partner.  他这场怀恨的诉讼显然是为了报复旧日的合作伙伴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 avenging</td><td>/əˈvendʒɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>复仇的，报复的</td><td>an avenging spirit<br>复仇的魂灵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He returned like an avenging figure from an old play.  他归来时像旧剧里复仇的角色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 resentful</td><td>/rɪˈzentfəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>愤恨的，不满的</td><td>resentful of criticism<br>对批评心怀不满</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She grew resentful when her contributions were repeatedly ignored.  当她的付出被一再忽视时，她开始心生怨恨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 retaliatory</td><td>/riˈtæljəˌtɔːri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>报复性的</td><td>retaliatory tariffs<br>报复性关税</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The country imposed retaliatory tariffs on farm goods.  该国对农产品加征了报复性关税。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 revengeful</td><td>/rɪˈvendʒfəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>报复心强的</td><td>a revengeful mood<br>报复的心态</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Losing the case left him bitter and revengeful.  败诉让他既苦涩又充满报复心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vengeful</td><td>/ˈvendʒfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>复仇心切的，图报复的</td><td>a vengeful rival<br>图报复的对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The vengeful neighbor cut down their shared hedge.  怀恨报复的邻居砍掉了两家共用的树篱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 forgiving</td><td>/fərˈɡɪvɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽容的，宽恕的</td><td>a forgiving nature<br>宽容的性格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was more forgiving than anyone expected her to be.  她比所有人预期的都更宽容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 merciful</td><td>/ˈmɜːrsɪfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>仁慈的，宽厚的</td><td>a merciful judge<br>宽仁的法官</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court was merciful and gave him only a small fine.  法庭很宽仁，只罚了他少许款项。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 relenting</td><td>/rɪˈlentɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>态度软化的，让步的</td><td>show a relenting attitude<br>表现出软化的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After hours of pleading, his relenting expression gave us hope.  在几小时的哀求之后，他态度软化的表情给了我们希望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 4

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>virtuoso</b></td><td>/ˌvɜːrtʃuˈoʊsoʊ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>技艺大师，演奏名家；技艺精湛的</td><td>a violin virtuoso<br>小提琴演奏大师</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The violin virtuoso played the difficult passage without a single mistake.  那位小提琴大师毫无差错地演奏了这段难曲。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adept</td><td>/əˈdept/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>内行的，熟练的；行家</td><td>adept at negotiation<br>擅长谈判</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is remarkably adept at calming angry customers.  她极擅长安抚愤怒的顾客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 connoisseur</td><td>/ˌkɑːnəˈsɜːr/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>鉴赏家（尤指艺术领域）；专家</td><td>a connoisseur of fine wine<br>美酒鉴赏家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is a connoisseur of fine wine and rare books.  他是美酒和珍本书的鉴赏家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 maestro</td><td>/ˈmaɪstroʊ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>大师（尤指音乐指挥家）</td><td>the aging maestro<br>年迈的大师</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The maestro raised his baton and the hall fell silent.  大师举起指挥棒，全场随之安静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 master</td><td>/ˈmæstər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>大师，名家；主人</td><td>a master of disguise<br>伪装大师</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only a master of the craft could carve such fine details.  只有行家高手才能雕出这般精细的细节。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 maven</td><td>/ˈmeɪvn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>专家</td><td>a marketing maven<br>营销专家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The magazine hired a marketing maven to explain why the campaign had failed.  杂志请来一位营销专家解释这次宣传为何失败。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 proficient</td><td>/prəˈfɪʃnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>熟练的，精通的</td><td>proficient in Spanish<br>精通西班牙语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After two years abroad she became proficient in Spanish and Portuguese.  在国外待了两年后，她精通了西班牙语和葡萄牙语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wizard</td><td>/ˈwɪzərd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>高手，奇才</td><td>a financial wizard<br>理财高手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team's wizard with numbers spotted the error instantly.  队里的数字高手一眼就看出了错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accomplished</td><td>/əˈkɑːmplɪʃt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>技艺精湛的，有造诣的</td><td>an accomplished pianist<br>技艺精湛的钢琴家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is an accomplished pianist who has toured three continents.  她是位技艺精湛的钢琴家，已在三大洲巡演。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consummate</td><td>/ˈkɑːnsəmət/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>专业的，有造诣的；无纰漏的，完美的</td><td>a consummate professional<br>极为专业的行家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She handled the hostile interview with the ease of a consummate diplomat.  她像老练的外交家一样从容应对了这场充满敌意的采访。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 experienced</td><td>/ɪkˈspɪriənst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有经验的，老练的</td><td>an experienced guide<br>经验丰富的导游</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We hired an experienced nurse to care for my grandmother.  我们请了一位有经验的护士照顾我祖母。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 masterful</td><td>/ˈmæstərfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>技艺精湛的，娴熟的</td><td>a masterful performance<br>一场技艺精湛的演出</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her masterful handling of the violin silenced the whole hall.  她精湛的小提琴技艺让全场肃静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 professed</td><td>/prəˈfest/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>自称的，公开声称的</td><td>a professed expert<br>自称的专家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professed authority could not answer a basic question.  这位自称的权威连基本问题都答不上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 skilled</td><td>/ˈskɪld/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>熟练的，有技能的</td><td>skilled labor<br>熟练劳动力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The factory struggles to find skilled workers for its new assembly line.  工厂难以为新装配线找到熟练工人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 versed</td><td>/vɜːrst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>熟知的；精通的</td><td>well versed in history<br>精通历史</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is well versed in both classical and modern poetry.  他对古典诗歌和现代诗歌都很精通。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 veteran</td><td>/ˈvetərən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>老兵；有丰富经验的人</td><td>a veteran journalist<br>资深记者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A veteran journalist, she has covered four wars and six elections.  作为资深记者，她报道过四场战争和六次选举。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 amateur</td><td>/ˈæməˌtɜːr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>业余爱好者；外行的</td><td>a talented amateur<br>有天赋的业余者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The comet was first spotted by an amateur astronomer.  这颗彗星最先由一位业余天文学家发现。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inexpert</td><td>/ˌɪnˈekspɜːrt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不熟练的，外行的</td><td>inexpert hands<br>不熟练的手法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 To inexpert eyes the two paintings looked exactly the same.  在外行眼里，这两幅画看起来完全一样。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inexperienced</td><td>/ˌɪnɪkˈspɪriənst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>缺乏经验的，不熟练的</td><td>an inexperienced driver<br>缺乏经验的司机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team was young and inexperienced but full of energy.  这支队伍年轻缺乏经验，却充满活力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unprofessional</td><td>/ˌʌnprəˈfeʃənəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不专业的，外行的</td><td>unprofessional conduct<br>不专业的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Arriving late to every meeting looks deeply unprofessional.  每次会议都迟到显得极不专业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unseasoned</td><td>/ʌnˈsizənd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>缺乏经验的；未调味的</td><td>unseasoned recruits<br>毫无经验的新兵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team fielded unseasoned players against the reigning champions.  球队派出毫无经验的球员迎战卫冕冠军。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unskilled</td><td>/ənˈskɪld/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不熟练的，无技术的</td><td>unskilled labor<br>非技术劳工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most of these jobs are unskilled and pay barely above the minimum.  这些工作多为非技术岗，薪水刚过最低标准。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unskillful</td><td>/ʌnˈskɪlfəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不熟练的，技艺拙劣的</td><td>unskillful workmanship<br>拙劣的手工活</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The unskillful repair left the table wobbling badly.  这次拙劣的修理让桌子摇晃得厉害。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>virtuous</b></td><td>/ˈvɜːrtʃuəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>品德高尚的，正直的</td><td>a virtuous life<br>高尚的生活</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He led a quiet, virtuous life and helped his neighbors freely.  他过着平静高尚的生活，乐于帮助邻里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decent</td><td>/ˈdiːsənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>得体的，正派的</td><td>decent and respectful behavior<br>得体而恭敬的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is a decent man who always keeps his promises.  他是个正派的人，总是守承诺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ethical</td><td>/ˈeθɪkəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>道德的，合乎道义的</td><td>ethical standards<br>道德标准</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Reporters must weigh ethical limits before publishing private letters.  记者发表私人信件前必须权衡道德界限。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 honest</td><td>/ˈɑːnəst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>诚实的，正直的</td><td>an honest answer<br>一个诚实的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Give me an honest answer, even if it disappoints me.  给我一个诚实的答复，即使会让我失望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 honorable</td><td>/ˈɑːnərəbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>光荣的，可敬的</td><td>an honorable retreat<br>光荣的撤退</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She made the honorable choice and returned all the money.  她做出可敬的选择，把钱全退了回去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 moral</td><td>/ˈmɔːrəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>道德的；道德准则</td><td>a moral obligation<br>道义上的义务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They felt a moral obligation to shelter the refugees.  他们觉得有道义上的义务收留难民。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 noble</td><td>/ˈnoʊbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>高贵的，贵族的</td><td>a noble bearing<br>高贵的气派</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The horse moved with a noble grace across the field.  那匹马以高贵的优雅姿态穿过田野。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 righteous</td><td>/ˈraɪtʃəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>正直的，正义的</td><td>righteous indignation<br>义愤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her righteous indignation at the ruling filled the courtroom.  她对判决的义愤充满法庭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 straight</td><td>/ˈstreɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>直的，笔直的</td><td>a straight road<br>笔直的道路</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Drive straight ahead until you reach the second traffic light.  一直往前开，直到第二个红绿灯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 upright</td><td>/ˈʌpraɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>正直的，诚实的；竖直的</td><td>an upright citizen<br>一位正直的公民</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Neighbors described him as an upright and reliable man.  邻居们说他是个正直可靠的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bad</td><td>/ˈbæd/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>恶意的；坏的，糟的</td><td>bad intentions<br>不良的居心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was no bad feeling between the two old rivals.  两位老对手之间并没有恶意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 evil</td><td>/ˈiːvəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>邪恶；邪恶的</td><td>the lesser evil<br>较小的祸害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel examines how ordinary people come to commit evil.  这部小说探讨普通人如何走向作恶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 immoral</td><td>/ɪˈmɔːrəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不道德的</td><td>an immoral bargain<br>不道德的交易</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Many considered the deal immoral even if it was legal.  许多人认为这笔交易即使合法也不道德。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 indecent</td><td>/ɪnˈdiːsnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不得体的，下流的</td><td>indecent language in public<br>公开场合的下流言语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The comedian was fined for using indecent language on live television.  这位喜剧演员因在直播电视上使用下流言语被罚款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sinful</td><td>/ˈsɪnfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有罪的，邪恶的</td><td>a sinful act<br>罪恶的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The preacher called greed the most sinful habit of our age.  牧师称贪婪是我们这个时代最罪恶的习性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unethical</td><td>/ʌnˈeθɪkəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不合道德准则的</td><td>unethical business practices<br>不合道德的商业做法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Hiding the test results was clearly unethical.  隐瞒检测结果显然不合道德。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unrighteous</td><td>/ʌnˈraɪtʃəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不正直的，邪恶的</td><td>unrighteous gain<br>不义之财</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sermon warned against unrighteous profit.  布道警告人们不要取不义之财。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wicked</td><td>/ˈwɪkɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>邪恶的</td><td>a wicked scheme<br>邪恶的计谋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A wicked scheme to seize the estate was uncovered by the lawyer.  律师揭穿了一个夺取遗产的邪恶计谋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wrong</td><td>/ˈrɔːŋ/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不道德的，错误的</td><td>morally wrong<br>道德上错误的</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Everyone agreed the decision was simply wrong.  所有人都认为那个决定根本就是错的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>virulent</b></td><td>/ˈvɪrələnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>恶毒的，充满敌意的；毒性强的</td><td>a virulent personal attack<br>恶毒的人身攻击</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The columnist launched a virulent attack on the new mayor.  这位专栏作家对新市长发起了恶毒的攻击。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 poisonous</td><td>/ˈpɔɪzənəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有毒的，恶毒的</td><td>a poisonous plant<br>有毒植物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Children were warned about the poisonous berries by the fence.  孩子们被告知篱笆边的浆果有毒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 toxic</td><td>/ˈtɑːksɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有毒的，有害的</td><td>toxic chemicals<br>有毒化学品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The factory dumped toxic waste into the river for years.  这家工厂多年向河里倾倒有毒废料。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 venomous</td><td>/ˈvenəməs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有毒的；恶毒的</td><td>a venomous snake<br>毒蛇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only two venomous snakes live on the whole island.  整个岛上只有两种毒蛇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 innocuous</td><td>/ɪˈnɑːkjuəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无害的；不会引起敌意的</td><td>an innocuous question<br>一个无恶意的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 What seemed an innocuous question turned out to reveal her deepest secret.  看似无恶意的问题最终却揭露了她最深的秘密。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>viscid</b></td><td>/ˈvɪsɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有粘性的</td><td>a viscid liquid<br>粘稠的液体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A viscid sap oozed slowly from the cut in the bark.  一种粘性的树液从树皮的切口缓缓渗出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adherent</td><td>/ədˈhɪrənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>追随者，拥护者</td><td>a devoted adherent<br>忠实的拥护者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The movement attracted thousands of adherents in a single year.  这场运动一年内就吸引了数千名拥护者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adhesive</td><td>/ædˈhiːsɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>粘性的；粘合剂</td><td>adhesive tape<br>胶带</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She sealed the heavy box with strong adhesive tape.  她用结实的胶带把重箱子封好。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clingy</td><td>/ˈklɪŋi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>黏的，黏附的</td><td>clingy wet sand<br>黏黏的湿沙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clingy mud held our boots at every step.  黏稠的泥每走一步都拽住我们的靴子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gluey</td><td>/ˈɡluːi/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>胶状的，粘的</td><td>a gluey substance<br>胶状的物质</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old pot was coated with a gluey brown substance.  旧锅里裹着一层棕色的胶状物质。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 glutinous</td><td>/ˈɡluːtənəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>胶状的，粘的</td><td>glutinous rice<br>糯米</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The glutinous rice stuck to my fingers while I ate.  我吃的时候糯米粘在了手指上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tenacious</td><td>/təˈneɪʃəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>黏性强的；不屈不挠的</td><td>a tenacious opponent<br>顽强的对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She proved a tenacious opponent who never conceded a single point.  她是个顽强的对手，从不让出一分。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 viscous</td><td>/ˈvɪskəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>粘稠的，粘的</td><td>a viscous liquid<br>粘稠的液体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The viscous liquid moved slowly down the inside of the jar.  这种粘稠的液体在罐子内壁上缓慢流下。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 slick</td><td>/ˈslɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>使光滑；光滑的</td><td>slick back one's hair<br>把头发抹平贴后</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He slicked back his hair before the interview.  面试前他把头发抹平贴后。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>viscous</b></td><td>/ˈvɪskəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>粘稠的，粘的</td><td>a viscous liquid<br>粘稠的液体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The viscous liquid moved slowly down the inside of the jar.  这种粘稠的液体在罐子内壁上缓慢流下。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 glutinous</td><td>/ˈɡluːtənəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>胶状的，粘的</td><td>glutinous rice<br>糯米</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The glutinous rice stuck to my fingers while I ate.  我吃的时候糯米粘在了手指上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 syrup</td><td>/ˈsɜːrəp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>糖浆，浓稠汁液</td><td>thick maple syrup<br>浓稠的枫糖浆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The syrup poured slowly over the warm pancakes.  糖浆缓缓浇在温热的薄饼上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 viscid</td><td>/ˈvɪsɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有粘性的</td><td>a viscid liquid<br>粘稠的液体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A viscid sap oozed slowly from the cut in the bark.  一种粘性的树液从树皮的切口缓缓渗出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fluid</td><td>/ˈfluːɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>易变的，不固定的；流体</td><td>a fluid situation<br>变动不定的局势</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The schedule remains fluid until the visas finally arrive.  在签证最终到手前，日程仍不固定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 watery</td><td>/ˈwɔːtəri/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>稀薄的，含水多的</td><td>watery soup<br>稀汤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The stew turned watery after he added too much broth.  他加汤太多，炖菜变得很稀。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>visionary</b></td><td>/ˈvɪʒəneri/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有远见的；不切实际的，空想的</td><td>a visionary founder<br>有远见的创始人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their visionary founder predicted the mobile market twenty years early.  他们有远见的创始人提前二十年预见了移动市场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 idealistic</td><td>/aɪˌdiːəˈlɪstɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>理想主义的</td><td>an idealistic student<br>理想主义的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their idealistic plan ignored every practical cost.  他们理想主义的计划忽略了一切实际成本。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 imaginary</td><td>/ɪˈmædʒəneri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>想象的，虚构的</td><td>an imaginary friend<br>想象出的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The boy blamed every mess on an imaginary friend.  男孩把每次弄乱都推给一个想象出的朋友。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impractical</td><td>/ˌɪmˈpræktəkəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不切实际的</td><td>an impractical plan<br>不切实际的计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Building a bridge there proved impractical and hugely expensive.  在那里建桥被证明不切实际且极其昂贵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quixotic</td><td>/kwɪkˈsɑːtɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不切实际的，空想的</td><td>a quixotic quest<br>不切实际的追寻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His quixotic quest to reform the entire system ended in failure.  他要改革整个体制的不切实际的追寻以失败告终。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 romantic</td><td>/roʊˈmæntɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不切实际的；浪漫的</td><td>a romantic notion<br>浪漫而不实的想法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He has a romantic notion that farming is always peaceful.  他有个浪漫想法，以为农耕总是宁静的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 utopian</td><td>/juˈtoʊpiən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>乌托邦式的，空想的</td><td>a utopian scheme<br>空想的方案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pamphlet proposed a utopian city without money or locks.  这本小册子设想了一座没有钱和锁的空想城市。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 farseeing</del></td><td>/ˌfɑːrˈsiːɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有远见的</td><td>a farseeing leader<br>有远见的领导者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A farseeing mayor planted these trees fifty years ago.  一位有远见的市长五十年前种下了这些树。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 farsighted</td><td>/ˈfɑːrsaɪtəd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有远见的；远视的</td><td>a farsighted policy<br>有远见的政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor's farsighted planning saved the city millions later.  市长有远见的规划后来为城市省下数百万。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 forehanded</del></td><td>/ˈfɔrˌhændɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有远见的；节俭的</td><td>forehanded planning<br>有远见的筹划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Forehanded farmers had already stored grain before the drought hit.  有远见的农民在旱灾来临前已存好粮食。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 foreseeing</td><td>/fɔːrˈsiːɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有先见之明的，预见的</td><td>a foreseeing planner<br>有远见的规划者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A foreseeing manager had stockpiled parts before the strike.  一位有先见之明的经理在罢工前囤了零件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 forethoughtful</del></td><td>/ˈfɔːrθɔːtfəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>深谋远虑的</td><td>a forethoughtful decision<br>深谋远虑的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One forethoughtful engineer insisted on a backup generator.  一位深谋远虑的工程师坚持要装备用发电机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prescient</td><td>/ˈpreʃiənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有先见之明的</td><td>a prescient warning<br>有先见之明的警告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His 1998 essay now looks remarkably prescient about the internet.  他一九九八年的文章如今看来对互联网极有先见之明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 proactive</td><td>/proʊˈæktɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>积极主动的，未雨绸缪的</td><td>a proactive approach<br>积极主动的做法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clinic takes a proactive approach to seasonal flu each autumn.  诊所每年秋天都对季节性流感采取主动措施。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 provident</td><td>/ˈprɑːvɪdənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>节俭的；有远见的</td><td>a provident household<br>节俭有远见的家庭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A provident household sets money aside long before hard times arrive.  节俭有远见的家庭远在困难到来之前就存下钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pragmatic</td><td>/præɡˈmætɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>实用主义的，务实的</td><td>a pragmatic approach<br>务实的做法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A pragmatic manager focuses on what works rather than on elegant theories.  务实的管理者关注什么有效，而不是漂亮的理论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 improvident</del></td><td>/ɪmˈprɑvɪdənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不节俭的；无远见的</td><td>improvident spending habits<br>不节俭的花钱习惯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of improvident borrowing left the family with nothing.  多年无节制的借贷让这家人一无所有。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 myopic</td><td>/maɪˈɑːpɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>缺乏远见的，缺乏辨别能力的</td><td>a myopic policy<br>缺乏远见的政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city's myopic policy on housing ignored problems that residents saw daily.  这座城市在住房上缺乏远见的政策忽视了居民每天都看得见的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 shortsighted</td><td>/ˈʃɔːrtsaɪtɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>短视的，缺乏远见的；近视的</td><td>a shortsighted decision<br>短视的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cutting research funding now would be extremely shortsighted.  现在削减研究经费是极其短视的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>vitality</b></td><td>/vaɪˈtæləti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>活力，生命力</td><td>brim with vitality<br>充满活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old market still brims with vitality on early summer mornings.  夏日清晨，这座老市场依然充满活力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 animation</td><td>/ˌænəˈmeɪʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>生气，活力</td><td>speak with animation<br>兴致勃勃地讲话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She described the trip with unusual animation and plenty of gestures.  她异常兴致勃勃地描述了那次旅行，还比划了许多手势。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bounce</td><td>/ˈbaʊns/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>驱逐，开除；弹跳</td><td>bounce him from the club<br>把他逐出俱乐部</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager bounced two waiters for stealing tips.  经理因偷小费开除了两名侍者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dynamism</td><td>/ˈdaɪnəˌmɪzəm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>活力，干劲</td><td>economic dynamism<br>经济活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city's dynamism attracts young workers every year.  这座城市的活力每年吸引年轻劳动者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 energy</td><td>/ˈenərdʒi/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>精力，活力；能量</td><td>full of restless energy<br>充满旺盛的精力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children still had plenty of energy after the long hike.  长途徒步之后孩子们仍精力充沛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 liveliness</td><td>/ˈlaɪvlinəs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>活力，生气</td><td>the liveliness of the market<br>市场的热闹活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The liveliness of the class surprised the visiting teacher.  班上的活力让来访的老师惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 robustness</td><td>/roʊˈbʌstnəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>强健，稳健</td><td>the robustness of the design<br>设计的稳健性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Engineers praised the robustness of the old bridge.  工程师赞赏这座旧桥的稳固。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 verve</td><td>/vɜːrv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>活力，热情</td><td>play with verve<br>充满激情地演奏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young pianist played that difficult passage with remarkable verve.  这位年轻钢琴家以非凡的热情演奏了那段高难度乐章。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vibrancy</td><td>/ˈvaɪbrənsi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>活力，生机</td><td>urban vibrancy<br>城市的活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New cafes restored some vibrancy to the old harbor.  新开的咖啡馆让老码头恢复了些活力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 vigorousness</del></td><td>/ˈvɪɡərəsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>精力充沛，强健</td><td>surprising vigorousness<br>出人意料的精力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At eighty he kept a vigorousness that shamed younger men.  八十岁的他仍精力充沛，让年轻人自愧不如。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vim</td><td>/vɪm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>活力，精力</td><td>full of vim and vigor<br>精力充沛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At seventy he still worked with surprising vim and good humor.  七十岁的他干活仍精力惊人，心情愉快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lethargy</td><td>/ˈleθərdʒi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>无生气，倦怠，昏睡</td><td>shake off his lethargy<br>摆脱他的倦怠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A short walk helped him shake off his afternoon lethargy.  短短的散步帮他摆脱了午后的倦怠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 listlessness</td><td>/ˈlɪstləsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>倦怠，无精打采</td><td>an air of listlessness<br>一副无精打采的样子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A strange listlessness settled over the office in August.  八月里办公室弥漫着一种奇怪的倦怠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sluggishness</td><td>/ˈslʌɡɪʃnəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>迟缓，无精打采</td><td>morning sluggishness<br>早晨的迟缓无力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The heat explained the general sluggishness of the crew.  炎热解释了船员们普遍的无精打采。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 torpidity</del></td><td>/tɔːrˈpɪdəti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>迟钝，麻木</td><td>sink into torpidity<br>陷入麻木迟钝</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Winter torpidity kept the frogs buried in the mud.  冬日的麻木状态让青蛙埋在泥中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>vitiate</b></td><td>/ˈvɪʃieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>削弱，损害；使无效</td><td>vitiate an agreement<br>使协议失效</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A single false statement can vitiate the entire contract.  一句虚假陈述就能使整份合同失效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blemish</td><td>/ˈblemɪʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>缺点，污点；损害，玷污</td><td>a blemish on his record<br>他履历上的污点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One late payment left a small blemish on his credit record.  一次逾期付款在他的信用记录上留下了小小的污点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cripple</td><td>/ˈkrɪpəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使严重受损，削弱</td><td>cripple the economy<br>严重削弱经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The strike crippled the city's transport system for a week.  罢工使该市交通系统瘫痪了一周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deface</td><td>/dɪˈfeɪs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>损毁外观，涂污</td><td>deface a monument<br>损毁纪念碑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Someone defaced the statue with spray paint during the night.  有人夜里用喷漆涂污了那座雕像。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 degrade</td><td>/dɪˈɡreɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>贬低，使降级；使退化</td><td>degrade the quality<br>降低质量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Such insulting jokes degrade everyone who repeats them.  这类侮辱性的笑话贬低了每个转述它们的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deteriorate</td><td>/dɪˈtɪriəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>恶化，变坏</td><td>deteriorate rapidly<br>迅速恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her health deteriorated sharply during the cold winter.  在寒冷的冬天里她的健康急剧恶化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flaw</td><td>/flɔː/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>瑕疵，缺点；降低，破坏，使不完美</td><td>a fatal flaw<br>致命的缺陷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One small flaw in the design caused the bridge to fail.  设计中一个小缺陷导致桥梁失效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 harm</td><td>/ˈhɑːrm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>伤害，损害</td><td>harm the environment<br>损害环境</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Pesticide runoff harms the fish living in the nearby stream.  农药径流危害了附近溪流中的鱼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impair</td><td>/ɪmˈper/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>损害，削弱</td><td>impair his judgment<br>削弱他的判断力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lack of sleep seriously impairs judgment and slows your reactions.  睡眠不足严重损害判断力并减慢反应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mar</td><td>/mɑːr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>破坏，削弱</td><td>mar the celebration<br>破坏庆典气氛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A careless remark at dinner managed to mar an otherwise perfect evening.  餐桌上一句轻率的话毁掉了本该完美的一晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 undermine</td><td>/ˈʌndərˌmaɪn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>削弱，暗中破坏</td><td>undermine confidence<br>削弱信心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Constant rumors undermined confidence in the young manager.  不断的谣言削弱了人们对这位年轻经理的信心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 doctor</td><td>/ˈdɑːktər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>篡改，窜改；医生</td><td>doctor the accounts<br>篡改账目</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was accused of doctoring the financial records last year.  他被指控篡改了去年的财务记录。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fix</td><td>/ˈfɪks/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>修理，解决；固定</td><td>fix the leaking pipe<br>修好漏水的管子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A neighbor fixed the leaking pipe before the floor was ruined.  邻居在地板毁坏前修好了漏水的管子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mend</td><td>/ˈmend/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>纠正，改正；修补</td><td>mend his ways<br>改正他的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He promised to mend his ways after the second stern warning.  第二次严厉警告后他承诺改正。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 patch</td><td>/ˈpætʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>斑块，小片；补丁</td><td>a damp patch<br>一块潮湿的斑迹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was a damp patch on the ceiling above the door.  门上方的天花板上有一块潮湿的斑迹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rebuild</td><td>/riˈbɪld/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>重建，修复</td><td>rebuild the bridge<br>重建桥梁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Volunteers helped rebuild the school after the flood.  志愿者帮助在洪水后重建学校。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 recondition</td><td>/ˌriːkənˈdɪʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>修复，翻新</td><td>recondition the engine<br>翻修发动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They recondition old bicycles and sell them at low prices.  他们翻新旧自行车并低价出售。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reconstruct</td><td>/ˌriːkənˈstrʌkt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>重建，重构；再现</td><td>reconstruct the crime scene<br>重现犯罪现场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Engineers reconstructed the bridge using the original nineteenth-century plans.  工程师们依照十九世纪的原始图纸重建了这座桥。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 renovate</td><td>/ˈrenəveɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>修复，维修</td><td>renovate the old house<br>翻修老房子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We must renovate the old house completely before moving in.  搬进去之前我们必须彻底翻修这栋老房子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 repair</td><td>/rɪˈper/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>修理，修补</td><td>repair the roof<br>修补屋顶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Workers repaired the roof just before the rainy season began.  工人们在雨季开始前修好了屋顶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 revamp</td><td>/riˈvæmp/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>改进，翻新，改造</td><td>revamp the website<br>改版网站</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company revamped its website to attract younger overseas customers.  公司改版网站以吸引更年轻的海外顾客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>vitriolic</b></td><td>/ˌvɪtriˈɑːlɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（言辞）刻薄的</td><td>a vitriolic attack<br>尖刻的攻击</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The review was so vitriolic that the author stopped writing.  那篇评论如此刻薄，作者因此停笔。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 acerbic</td><td>/əˈsɜːrbɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（语调）尖酸的，刻薄的</td><td>acerbic wit<br>尖刻的机智</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reviewer is famous for her acerbic comments on new plays.  这位评论家以对新剧的尖酸评论闻名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 acid</td><td>/ˈæsəd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>尖刻的；酸的；酸</td><td>an acid remark<br>一句尖刻的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her acid comment about his writing silenced the whole table.  她对他文章的尖刻评论让全桌人都沉默了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 acrid</td><td>/ˈækrɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>刻薄的；辛辣刺鼻的</td><td>acrid smoke<br>刺鼻的烟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An acrid smell of burning plastic filled the hallway.  一股塑料烧焦的刺鼻气味充满了走廊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bitter</td><td>/ˈbɪtər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>严寒刺骨的；苦的</td><td>a bitter north wind<br>刺骨的北风</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We waited an hour in bitter cold for the train.  我们在刺骨的严寒中等了一小时火车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 biting</td><td>/ˈbaɪtɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>尖刻的，辛辣的</td><td>biting sarcasm<br>尖刻的讽刺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His biting review ended the young author's confidence.  他尖刻的评论摧毁了那位年轻作者的信心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 caustic</td><td>/ˈkɔːstɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>挖苦讽刺的，刻薄的</td><td>a caustic remark<br>刻薄的评论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His caustic remarks about the film upset the young director.  他对影片刻薄的评论让年轻导演很难过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 corrosive</td><td>/kəˈroʊsɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>腐蚀性的；讽刺性的</td><td>a corrosive chemical<br>腐蚀性化学品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Salt water is highly corrosive and slowly eats through the ship's hull.  盐水腐蚀性很强，会慢慢蚀穿船体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 harsh</td><td>/hɑːrʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>严厉的</td><td>harsh criticism<br>严厉的批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge's harsh sentence surprised even the prosecutors in the courtroom.  法官严厉的判决连法庭上的检察官都感到意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mordant</td><td>/ˈmɔːrdnt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>尖酸刻薄的</td><td>mordant wit<br>尖刻的机智</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His mordant wit amused readers but cost him several close friendships.  他尖刻的机智取悦了读者，却让他失去几段亲密友谊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scalding</td><td>/ˈskɔːldɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>滚烫的；（言辞）严厉的</td><td>scalding criticism<br>严厉的批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The critic wrote a scalding review of the opening night.  评论家为首演之夜写了一篇严厉的评论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scathing</td><td>/ˈskeɪðɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>尖酸刻薄的，严厉的</td><td>a scathing attack<br>尖刻的攻击</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She made a scathing remark about his sloppy research.  她对他草率的研究作了一句尖刻的评论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sharp</td><td>/ˈʃɑːrp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>尖刻的；锋利的；敏锐的</td><td>a sharp tongue<br>说话尖刻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The critic has a sharp tongue but an unusually fair mind.  这位评论家言辞尖刻，心思却格外公正。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tart</td><td>/ˈtɑːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>尖刻的；酸的</td><td>a tart reply<br>尖刻的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her tart reply silenced everyone at the dinner table.  她尖刻的回答让餐桌上所有人都沉默了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 balmy</td><td>/ˈbɑːmi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>温和宜人的</td><td>a balmy evening<br>温暖宜人的傍晚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We sat outside enjoying the balmy air of early summer.  我们坐在外面享受初夏温和宜人的空气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 benign</td><td>/bɪˈnaɪn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无害的</td><td>a benign influence<br>有益无害的影响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The change in policy proved benign for most small farmers.  事实证明，政策变化对大多数小农户是无害的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bland</td><td>/blænd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无趣的</td><td>a bland performance<br>平淡的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The critics called it a bland performance with no real feeling.  评论家称这是一场毫无真情、十分无趣的表演。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 delicate</td><td>/ˈdeləkət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>微妙的，精细的；脆弱的</td><td>a delicate distinction<br>微妙的区别</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has a delicate ear for the subtle differences between dialects.  她对方言之间的细微差别有很灵敏的耳朵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 light</td><td>/ˈlaɪt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻微的，不严重的</td><td>a light meal<br>一顿便餐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He suffered only light injuries in the accident.  他在事故中只受了轻伤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mellow</td><td>/ˈmeloʊ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>温和的，圆熟的；变温和</td><td>grow mellow with age<br>随年岁渐趋温和</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He has grown much mellower since he became a grandfather.  当上祖父后他变得温和多了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mild</td><td>/ˈmaɪld/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>温和的，不严厉的</td><td>a mild punishment<br>轻微的处罚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The winter here is unusually mild and rarely brings snow.  这里的冬天格外温和，很少下雪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 nonabrasive</del></td><td>/ˌnɑnəˈbreɪsɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>温和的，不刺人的</td><td>a nonabrasive tone<br>温和的语气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave the criticism in a nonabrasive, friendly tone.  她以温和友善的语气提出批评。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 soft</td><td>/ˈsɑːft/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>柔和的，轻微的</td><td>a soft voice<br>轻柔的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke in a soft tone so the baby would not wake.  她轻声说话，以免把婴儿弄醒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 soothing</td><td>/ˈsuːðɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>抚慰的，缓解疼痛的</td><td>a soothing ointment<br>一种止痛药膏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She played soothing music to help the baby fall asleep.  她放着舒缓的音乐帮助婴儿入睡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tender</td><td>/ˈtendər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>温柔的，心软同情的；正式提出</td><td>a tender heart<br>一颗温柔同情的心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has a tender heart and cannot bear to see animals suffer.  她心地温软，见不得动物受苦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>vituperate</b></del></td><td>/vaɪˈtuːpəreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>谩骂，责骂</td><td>vituperate an opponent<br>谩骂对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The columnist vituperated the mayor in every weekly article.  这位专栏作家在每周的文章里都痛骂市长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abuse</td><td>/əˈbjuːz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>辱骂，抨击；不正当或不合理使用</td><td>abuse of power<br>滥用权力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was accused of abusing his position for profit.  他被指控滥用职位谋利。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assail</td><td>/əˈseɪl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抨击，严厉批评</td><td>assail a policy<br>抨击某项政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Opponents assailed the plan as reckless and unfair.  反对者抨击该计划轻率且不公。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 belabor</td><td>/bɪˈleɪbər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>过分强调；痛打</td><td>belabor the point<br>反复纠缠一点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is no need to belabor the point any further.  没有必要再对这一点过分强调下去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 berate</td><td>/bɪˈreɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>严厉指责，斥责</td><td>berate him publicly<br>公开斥责他</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach began to berate his players in front of reporters.  教练开始在记者面前严厉斥责他的球员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 castigate</td><td>/ˈkæstɪɡeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>强烈（公开）指责</td><td>castigate for negligence<br>因疏忽而受斥责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge castigated the company for its negligence.  法官严厉指责该公司的疏忽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 excoriate</td><td>/ɪkˈskɔːrieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>严厉批评；擦伤皮肤</td><td>excoriate the government<br>严厉批评政府</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics excoriated the film for its casual cruelty.  批评家严厉批评该片的随意残忍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lambaste</td><td>/læmˈbeɪst/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>严厉斥责，痛骂</td><td>lambaste the government<br>痛斥政府</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics lambasted the film for its shallow, sentimental ending.  批评家严厉斥责这部电影结局浅薄而煽情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reprimand</td><td>/ˈreprəˌmænd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>训斥，正式谴责</td><td>reprimand an employee<br>训斥员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The officer was reprimanded for leaving his post early.  这名警官因提早离岗而受到训斥。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scold</td><td>/ˈskoʊld/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>责骂，训斥</td><td>scold the children<br>责骂孩子们</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher scolded the boys for running through the hallway.  老师因男孩们在走廊跑动而训斥他们。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rail</td><td>/reɪl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>痛斥，抱怨</td><td>rail against injustice<br>猛烈抨击不公</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students railed against the unfair new grading policy all week.  学生们整周都在痛斥不公平的新评分政策。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 revile</td><td>/riˈvaɪl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>辱骂，斥责</td><td>revile his opponents<br>辱骂对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Newspapers reviled the mayor for weeks after the scandal broke.  丑闻曝光后报纸辱骂市长数周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 upbraid</del></td><td>/ʌpˈbreɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（严厉地）谴责，责骂</td><td>upbraid him for lying<br>因撒谎而责骂他</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager upbraided him for missing three deadlines in one month.  经理因他一个月内三次误期而严厉责骂他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 admire</td><td>/ədˈmaɪr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>钦佩，赞赏</td><td>admire her courage<br>钦佩她的勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I admire the way she handled such a difficult situation.  我钦佩她处理如此棘手局面的方式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 commend</td><td>/kəˈmend/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞扬，表扬</td><td>commend her courage<br>赞扬她的勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The principal commended her courage in front of the whole school.  校长在全校面前赞扬了她的勇气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 laud</td><td>/ˈlɔːd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美，称赞</td><td>laud his courage<br>赞扬他的勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics lauded the young pianist for her fearless interpretation.  评论家赞扬这位年轻钢琴家大胆的演绎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 praise</td><td>/ˈpreɪz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>赞扬，称赞</td><td>win high praise<br>获得高度赞扬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her first novel won high praise from serious critics.  她的第一部小说赢得了严肃评论家的高度赞扬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 5

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>vivacious</b></td><td>/vɪˈveɪʃəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>活泼的，快活的</td><td>a vivacious personality<br>活泼的性格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her vivacious manner made every stranger feel welcome at the table.  她活泼的举止让每个陌生人在餐桌旁都感到受欢迎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 active</td><td>/ˈæktɪv/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>活跃的，活动的</td><td>an active volcano<br>活火山</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She stays active by swimming every morning.  她每天早上游泳以保持活跃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 animated</td><td>/ˈænəˌmeɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>活跃的，生气勃勃的</td><td>an animated discussion<br>一场热烈的讨论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The class turned into an animated debate about ethics.  这堂课变成了一场关于伦理的热烈辩论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bouncing</td><td>/ˈbaʊnsɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>活泼健康的，精力充沛的</td><td>a bouncing baby boy<br>健康活泼的男婴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave birth to a bouncing baby boy last spring.  去年春天她生下一个健康活泼的男婴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 brisk</td><td>/brɪsk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满生机的，有活力的</td><td>a brisk walk<br>快步散步</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A brisk walk before breakfast leaves me alert all morning.  早餐前快走一趟能让我整个上午都很清醒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 energetic</td><td>/ˌenərˈdʒetɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>精力充沛的</td><td>an energetic performance<br>活力四射的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Even at seventy she remains remarkably energetic and full of plans.  即使七十岁了，她依然精力充沛，计划满满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 frisky</td><td>/ˈfrɪski/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>活泼欢腾的</td><td>a frisky puppy<br>一只活泼的小狗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The frisky colt raced across the field at dawn.  黎明时那匹活泼的小马驹在田野上奔跑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 kinetic</td><td>/kəˈnetɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满活力的；运动的</td><td>kinetic energy<br>动能</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dancer's kinetic style filled the stage with motion.  这位舞者充满动感的风格让舞台生机勃勃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 mettlesome</del></td><td>/ˈmɛtəlsəm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>精神饱满的，勇猛的</td><td>a mettlesome young horse<br>精神饱满的小马</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mettlesome colt refused to stand still.  这匹精神饱满的小马驹不肯站定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spirited</td><td>/ˈspɪrɪtɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>生气勃勃的，活跃的</td><td>a spirited debate<br>一场热烈的辩论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The candidates held a spirited debate about tax policy.  候选人就税收政策展开了热烈的辩论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sprightly</td><td>/ˈspraɪtli/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>活泼的，充满活力的</td><td>a sprightly old man<br>一位活泼的老人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sprightly old man led the hikers up the steep mountain path.  一位活泼的老人带着徒步者爬上陡峭的山路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vital</td><td>/ˈvaɪtəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满活力的；极重要的</td><td>full of vital energy<br>充满生命活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Regular exercise keeps older people vital and mentally alert.  经常锻炼使老年人保持活力和头脑清醒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 zippy</td><td>/ˈzɪpi/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>活泼的，充满活力的</td><td>a zippy little tune<br>轻快活泼的小曲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The zippy opening number woke the sleepy audience.  活泼的开场曲唤醒了昏昏欲睡的观众。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dead</td><td>/ˈded/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>死的；毫无生气的</td><td>a dead town at night<br>夜里死寂的小镇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The resort is completely dead in the winter months.  这个度假地在冬季几个月里毫无生气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inactive</td><td>/ɪnˈæktɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不活动的，不活跃的</td><td>remain inactive for months<br>数月毫无动作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The volcano has been inactive for over two hundred years.  这座火山已有两百多年没有活动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inanimate</td><td>/ɪnˈænɪmət/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无生命的</td><td>an inanimate object<br>无生命的物体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Children often speak to inanimate objects as though they could reply.  孩子常对无生命的物体说话，仿佛它们能够回答。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 languid</td><td>/ˈlæŋɡwɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>没精打采的，虚弱的</td><td>a languid summer afternoon<br>慢悠悠的夏日午后</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A languid summer afternoon passed with nobody willing to leave the shade.  一个慢悠悠的夏日午后过去了，没人愿意离开树荫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 languorous</td><td>/ˈlæŋɡərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>倦怠的，无精打采的</td><td>a languorous afternoon<br>倦懒的午后</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They spent a languorous afternoon lying under the fig tree.  他们在无花果树下度过一个倦懒的午后。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 leaden</td><td>/ˈledən/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>沉重的，无生气的；铅灰的</td><td>a leaden sky<br>铅灰色的天空</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He walked home with leaden steps after the painful defeat.  痛苦的失败后，他步履沉重地走回家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lifeless</td><td>/ˈlaɪfləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无生命的；无生气的</td><td>a lifeless performance<br>毫无生气的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The actors gave a lifeless performance to a bored audience.  演员们在无聊的观众前演得毫无生气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 listless</td><td>/ˈlɪstləs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无精打采的，倦怠的</td><td>feel listless and tired<br>感到倦怠疲乏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fever left her listless for nearly a week.  发烧让她将近一周都无精打采。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 spiritless</del></td><td>/ˈspɪrɪtləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无精打采的，没有生气的</td><td>a spiritless performance<br>毫无生气的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team gave a spiritless performance in the second half.  球队在下半场表现得毫无生气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vapid</td><td>/ˈvæpɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无趣的，乏味的</td><td>a vapid conversation<br>乏味的谈话</td></tr>
