<tr><td colspan="7">例 The party ended in vapid conversation about traffic and the weather.  聚会最后只剩下关于交通和天气的乏味闲谈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>vociferous</b></td><td>/voʊˈsɪfərəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>喧哗的，大叫大嚷的</td><td>a vociferous critic<br>大声抗议的批评者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She became a vociferous opponent of the new tax law.  她成了新税法的大声反对者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blatant</td><td>/ˈbleɪtnt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明显的，公然的</td><td>a blatant lie<br>赤裸裸的谎言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report exposed a blatant disregard for safety rules.  报告揭露了对安全规则的公然无视。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 boisterous</td><td>/ˈbɔɪstərəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>喧嚷的，吵闹的</td><td>a boisterous crowd<br>喧闹的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A boisterous crowd filled the square outside the stadium.  一群喧闹的人挤满了体育场外的广场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 clamant</del></td><td>/ˈkleɪmənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>迫切的；喧闹的</td><td>a clamant need<br>迫切的需要</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is a clamant demand for clean drinking water here.  这里对清洁饮用水有迫切的需求。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clamorous</td><td>/ˈklæmərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>喧闹的；大声要求的</td><td>clamorous demands for reform<br>高声要求改革</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clamorous market made quiet conversation impossible.  喧闹的市场让人根本无法安静交谈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obstreperous</td><td>/əbˈstrepərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不听话的，任性的</td><td>an obstreperous child<br>不听话的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An obstreperous child kept kicking the seat in front of him.  一个吵闹不驯的孩子一直踢着前排的座椅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 strident</td><td>/ˈstraɪdnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>刺耳的</td><td>a strident voice<br>刺耳的嗓音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her strident voice cut through the noise of the crowded hall.  她刺耳的嗓音穿透拥挤大厅里的喧闹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 yowling</td><td>/ˈjaʊlɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>大声嚎叫的</td><td>yowling cats<br>嚎叫的猫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Yowling cats kept the whole street awake till dawn.  嚎叫的猫让整条街到天亮都睡不着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reticent</td><td>/ˈretɪsnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>沉默不语的</td><td>reticent about his past<br>对往事讳莫如深</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was oddly reticent about the years he spent working abroad.  他对自己在国外工作的那些年出奇地沉默不语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 taciturn</td><td>/ˈtæsɪtɜːrn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>沉默寡言的，话少的</td><td>a taciturn farmer<br>沉默寡言的农夫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The taciturn farmer answered our questions with a nod or a shrug.  沉默寡言的农夫只用点头或耸肩回答我们的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>volatile</b></td><td>/ˈvɑːlətl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>多变的</td><td>a volatile stock market<br>多变的股市</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Investors hesitated because the stock market stayed volatile all spring.  由于股市整个春天都动荡多变，投资者犹豫不决。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 capricious</td><td>/kəˈprɪʃəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>反复无常的，善变的</td><td>capricious weather<br>变化无常的天气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The capricious wind kept shifting during the whole race.  整场比赛中变化无常的风一直在转向。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 changeable</td><td>/ˈtʃeɪndʒəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易变的，可改变的</td><td>changeable weather<br>多变的天气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The weather in April is notoriously changeable here.  这里四月的天气以多变著称。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fluid</td><td>/ˈfluːɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>易变的，不固定的；流体</td><td>a fluid situation<br>变动不定的局势</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The schedule remains fluid until the visas finally arrive.  在签证最终到手前，日程仍不固定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inconstant</td><td>/ɪnˈkɑːnstənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易变的，反复无常的</td><td>an inconstant friend<br>反复无常的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The inconstant wind made sailing across the bay nearly impossible.  变化不定的风让横渡海湾几乎无法进行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mercurial</td><td>/mɜːrˈkjʊriəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（情绪）善变的</td><td>a mercurial temper<br>善变的脾气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His mercurial temper made colleagues hesitate before bringing him any bad news.  他善变的脾气让同事在向他报告坏消息前都要犹豫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 skittish</td><td>/ˈskɪtɪʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>容易受到惊吓的，胆小的；善变的，多变的</td><td>a skittish horse<br>易受惊的马</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The skittish horse bolted the moment it heard the sudden thunder.  那匹易受惊的马一听到突然的雷声就狂奔起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 temperamental</td><td>/ˌtemprəˈmentəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>喜怒无常的；不稳定的</td><td>a temperamental artist<br>喜怒无常的艺术家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old printer is temperamental on humid summer days.  这台旧打印机在潮湿的夏日很不稳定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 variable</td><td>/ˈveriəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>可变的，多变的</td><td>variable costs<br>可变成本</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Winds in the strait are variable and often dangerous.  海峡里的风多变且常常危险。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 versatile</td><td>/ˈvɜːrsətəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>多才多艺的，全能的</td><td>a versatile performer<br>多才多艺的表演者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A versatile performer, she sings, dances, and plays three instruments.  作为多才多艺的表演者，她能唱歌、跳舞并演奏三种乐器。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 certain</td><td>/ˈsɜːrtən/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>确定的，无疑的</td><td>certain of the outcome<br>对结果有把握</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We are certain that the letter was never sent.  我们确信那封信从未寄出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 changeless</td><td>/ˈtʃeɪndʒləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不变的，永恒的</td><td>a changeless landscape<br>恒久不变的景色</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The village seemed changeless, though the world outside had transformed completely.  村子好像从未改变，尽管外面的世界已彻底变样。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 constant</td><td>/ˈkɑːnstənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>不变的，恒定的；忠诚的</td><td>a constant friend<br>忠诚不变的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her support remained constant through every difficult year.  她的支持在每个艰难年头都不曾改变。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 immutable</td><td>/ɪˈmjuːtəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可变的</td><td>an immutable law of nature<br>不可变的自然法则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Gravity is an immutable law that no clever argument can suspend.  引力是不可变的法则，任何巧辩都无法使它失效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 invariable</td><td>/ɪnˈveriəbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不变的，恒定的</td><td>an invariable routine<br>一成不变的常规</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His morning routine has been invariable for twenty years.  他的晨间习惯二十年来一成不变。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 settled</td><td>/ˈsetəld/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>固定的，安定的</td><td>a settled routine<br>固定的作息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After years of moving they finally led a settled life.  多年漂泊后他们终于过上安定的生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stable</td><td>/ˈsteɪbəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>稳定的；马厩</td><td>a stable income<br>稳定的收入</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His condition has been stable since the operation last Tuesday.  自上周二手术以来他的病情一直稳定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stationary</td><td>/ˈsteɪʃəˌneri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>静止的，固定不动的</td><td>remain stationary<br>保持静止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Traffic on the bridge stayed stationary for almost two hours.  桥上的车流几乎两小时一动不动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 steady</td><td>/ˈstedi/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>稳定的，持续不变的</td><td>a steady stream of visitors<br>络绎不绝的来访者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sales have shown steady growth for eight straight quarters.  销售额已连续八个季度稳定增长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unchangeable</td><td>/ʌnˈtʃeɪndʒəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可改变的</td><td>unchangeable natural laws<br>不变的自然法则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He treated his own opinions as unchangeable truths.  他把自己的观点当作不可改变的真理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unvarying</td><td>/ʌnˈvɛriɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无变化的，始终一致的</td><td>an unvarying rhythm<br>一成不变的节奏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The machine repeated the task at an unvarying speed all night.  机器整夜以一成不变的速度重复这项工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>volition</b></td><td>/vəˈlɪʃn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>意志，自愿选择的行为</td><td>of his own volition<br>出于他自己的意愿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He resigned of his own volition, without pressure from the board.  他出于自己的意愿辞职，并未受到董事会的压力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 choice</td><td>/ˈtʃɔɪs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>选择，抉择</td><td>a free choice<br>自由的选择</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She made the choice without consulting her family.  她没和家人商量就作了这个选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decision</td><td>/dɪˈsɪʒən/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>决定，决断</td><td>make a decision<br>作出决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The decision to sell the farm was entirely his own.  卖掉农场的决定完全是他自己作的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 discretion</td><td>/dɪˈskreʃn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>谨慎；自制；自行决定权</td><td>at your own discretion<br>由你自行决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 You may spend the remaining funds at your own discretion this quarter.  本季度剩余的资金你可自行决定如何使用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 will</td><td>/ˈwɪl/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>意志，意愿</td><td>against her will<br>违背她的意愿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No one may be held here against his will.  任何人不得被违背意愿地留在此处。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 coercion</td><td>/koʊˈɜːrʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>强迫，胁迫</td><td>under coercion<br>在胁迫之下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The confession was obtained by clear coercion.  这份供词明显是靠胁迫取得的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 compulsion</td><td>/kəmˈpʌlʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>强制，强迫</td><td>act under compulsion<br>在强迫下行事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He signed under compulsion and later withdrew consent.  他在强迫下签字，后来撤回了同意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 constraint</td><td>/kənˈstreɪnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>限制，约束；拘束</td><td>budget constraints<br>预算限制</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Time constraints forced the team to abandon several experiments.  时间限制迫使团队放弃了几项实验。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 duress</td><td>/dʊˈres/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>强迫，胁迫</td><td>confess under duress<br>在胁迫下认罪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contract is void if it was signed under duress.  若合同是在胁迫下签署的则属无效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 force</td><td>/ˈfɔːrs/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>强迫，迫使；力量</td><td>force someone to confess<br>迫使某人认罪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The heavy rain forced them to cancel the outdoor ceremony.  大雨迫使他们取消了户外典礼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pressure</td><td>/ˈpreʃər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>压力，强迫</td><td>political pressure<br>政治压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Under pressure from lenders the family sold the land.  在债权人的压力下这家人卖掉了土地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>voluble</b></td><td>/ˈvɑːljəbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>健谈的，话多的</td><td>a voluble speaker<br>健谈的讲话者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our voluble guide talked cheerfully about every stone in the old city.  我们健谈的导游兴致勃勃地讲述古城里的每一块石头。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chatty</td><td>/ˈtʃæti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>健谈的，爱聊天的</td><td>a chatty neighbor<br>健谈的邻居</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our chatty neighbor kept us at the door for an hour.  健谈的邻居让我们在门口站了一小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 eloquent</td><td>/ˈeləkwənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>雄辩的，有说服力的</td><td>an eloquent speech<br>雄辩的演讲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His eloquent defense of the plan won several extra votes.  他对该计划雄辩的辩护多赢得了几张选票。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 garrulous</td><td>/ˈɡærələs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>啰嗦的，话多令人厌烦的</td><td>a garrulous neighbor<br>唠叨的邻居</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our garrulous neighbor talked for an hour about his lawn.  我们唠叨的邻居就他的草坪讲了一个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 glib</td><td>/ɡlɪb/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>油腔滑调的；肤浅的</td><td>a glib salesman<br>油腔滑调的销售员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His glib answers convinced no one on the hiring committee that afternoon.  那天下午他油腔滑调的回答没能说服招聘委员会的任何人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 loquacious</td><td>/ləˈkweɪʃəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>话多的</td><td>a loquacious guide<br>话多的导游</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our loquacious guide talked steadily from the museum door to the exit.  我们话多的导游从博物馆门口一直讲到出口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 talkative</td><td>/ˈtɔːkətɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>健谈的，爱说话的</td><td>a talkative passenger<br>健谈的乘客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our talkative driver described every building along the winding coastal road.  健谈的司机介绍了蜿蜒海滨路上的每座建筑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vocative</td><td>/ˈvɑkətɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>呼唤的，称呼的；呼格的</td><td>the vocative case<br>呼格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Latin uses a vocative form when directly addressing a person.  拉丁语直接称呼某人时使用呼格形式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reticent</td><td>/ˈretɪsnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>沉默不语的</td><td>reticent about his past<br>对往事讳莫如深</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was oddly reticent about the years he spent working abroad.  他对自己在国外工作的那些年出奇地沉默不语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 taciturn</td><td>/ˈtæsɪtɜːrn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>沉默寡言的，话少的</td><td>a taciturn farmer<br>沉默寡言的农夫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The taciturn farmer answered our questions with a nod or a shrug.  沉默寡言的农夫只用点头或耸肩回答我们的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>voluminous</b></td><td>/vəˈluːmɪnəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>卷数多的，大量的，庞大的</td><td>voluminous records<br>卷帙浩繁的记录</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyer arrived with voluminous records of every payment ever made.  律师带着关于每一笔付款的浩繁记录到场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 colossal</td><td>/kəˈlɑːsl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>巨大的</td><td>a colossal statue<br>巨大的雕像</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A colossal statue of the founder stands in the square.  广场上矗立着创始人的巨大雕像。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 considerable</td><td>/kənˈsɪdərəbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（数量上）可观（而值得注意）的</td><td>a considerable sum<br>一笔可观的钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The renovation required a considerable amount of money and nearly two years of work.  这次翻修耗费了可观的资金和近两年的工期。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 elephantine</td><td>/ˌelɪˈfæntiːn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>巨大的；笨拙的</td><td>elephantine movements<br>笨重的动作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His elephantine movements on stage made the delicate dance look almost comic.  他在台上笨重的动作让这支细腻的舞蹈显得近乎滑稽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enormous</td><td>/ɪˈnɔːrməs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>巨大的，庞大的</td><td>an enormous amount of data<br>庞大的数据量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The project required an enormous amount of money and patience.  这个项目需要巨额资金和极大耐心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gargantuan</td><td>/ɡɑːrˈɡæntʃuən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>巨大的</td><td>a gargantuan appetite<br>巨大的食量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young athlete has a gargantuan appetite after every practice.  这名年轻运动员每次训练后都有巨大的食量。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gigantic</td><td>/dʒaɪˈɡæntɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>巨大的</td><td>a gigantic stone statue<br>巨大的石像</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A gigantic stone statue guards the entrance to the temple.  一座巨大的石像守卫着神庙的入口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mammoth</td><td>/ˈmæməθ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>巨大的；猛犸象</td><td>a mammoth task<br>艰巨的任务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rebuilding the port after the storm was a mammoth undertaking.  风暴后重建港口是一项巨大的工程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 numerous</td><td>/ˈnuːmərəs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>众多的，许多的</td><td>on numerous occasions<br>在许多场合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has warned us about this on numerous occasions.  她在许多场合就此警告过我们。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 oversize</td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈsaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>特大的，过大的</td><td>an oversize coat<br>特大号外套</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He arrived in an oversize jacket borrowed from his brother.  他穿着从哥哥那借来的特大号夹克来了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 scanty</td><td>/ˈskænti/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>缺乏的，稀少的</td><td>scanty evidence<br>证据不足</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Evidence for the theory remains scanty.  支持该理论的证据仍然很少。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 scarce</td><td>/ˈskers/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>稀缺的，不足的</td><td>in scarce supply<br>供应短缺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fresh water became scarce during the drought.  旱灾期间淡水变得稀缺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>voluptuous</b></td><td>/vəˈlʌptʃuəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>奢侈逸乐的，沉溺酒色的</td><td>a voluptuous lifestyle<br>奢侈逸乐的生活</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He abandoned his studies for a voluptuous life of parties and wine.  他放弃学业，过上了纵情享乐、酒会不断的生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 carnal</td><td>/ˈkɑːrnl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>肉体的，物质的；世俗的</td><td>carnal pleasures<br>肉体的享乐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monk warned his students against carnal pleasures and idle talk.  僧人告诫学生远离肉体享乐和闲谈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 epicurean</td><td>/ˌepəkjʊˈriːən/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>享乐的，讲究口腹之欲的</td><td>epicurean tastes<br>讲究享乐的口味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His epicurean tastes made every dinner an expensive event.  他讲究享乐的口味让每顿晚餐都花费不菲。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 luscious</td><td>/ˈlʌʃəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>甘美的，甜美诱人的</td><td>luscious ripe peaches<br>甘美的熟桃</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The market sold luscious peaches picked that morning.  市场上卖着当天早上采的甘美桃子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lush</td><td>/lʌʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>茂盛的，繁茂的</td><td>lush vegetation<br>茂盛的植被</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lush vegetation covered the valley floor after weeks of steady rain.  连续数周的降雨之后，茂盛的植被覆盖了谷底。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 luxurious</td><td>/lʌɡˈʒʊriəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>奢侈的</td><td>a luxurious hotel suite<br>豪华的酒店套房</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They spent their anniversary in a luxurious hotel suite overlooking the harbor.  他们在一间俯瞰港口的豪华酒店套房里度过了结婚纪念日。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 indulgent</td><td>/ɪnˈdʌldʒənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（对己）放纵的，（对他人）纵容的</td><td>an indulgent parent<br>溺爱孩子的家长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her indulgent grandparents let her eat whatever she wanted.  溺爱她的祖父母让她想吃什么就吃什么。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sensual</td><td>/ˈsenʃuəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>感官的，纵情声色的</td><td>sensual pleasures<br>感官的享乐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He abandoned scholarship for a life of sensual pleasure.  他放弃学术，投入声色享乐的生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sensuous</td><td>/ˈsenʃuəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>感官的，愉悦感官的</td><td>sensuous pleasure<br>感官的享乐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poem dwells on the sensuous detail of ripe fruit.  这首诗沉溺于熟果的感官细节。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ascetic</td><td>/əˈsetɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>苦行的，禁欲的；苦行者</td><td>an ascetic lifestyle<br>苦修的生活方式</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monk led an ascetic life on bread and cold water.  这位僧人以面包和冷水过着自我克制的生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 spartan</td><td>/ˈspɑːrtn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>简朴的，节约的</td><td>spartan living conditions<br>简朴的生活条件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monks lived in spartan rooms with only a bed and a lamp.  僧侣们住在只有一床一灯的简朴房间里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 self-denying</td><td>/ˌself dɪˈnaɪɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>克己的，自我节制的</td><td>a self-denying habit<br>克己的习惯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His self-denying routine allowed no sweets at all.  他克己的作息完全不允许甜食。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>voracious</b></td><td>/vəˈreɪʃəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>贪吃的；如饥似渴的</td><td>a voracious appetite<br>极大的食欲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is a voracious reader who finishes a novel each week.  他是个如饥似渴的读者，每周读完一本小说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 edacious</del></td><td>/ɪˈdeɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贪吃的，暴食的</td><td>an edacious appetite<br>贪吃的胃口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The edacious puppy emptied both bowls in a minute.  贪吃的小狗一分钟就吃空了两个碗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 esurient</del></td><td>/ɪˈsʊriənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>饥饿的，贪食的</td><td>esurient guests<br>饥肠辘辘的客人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Esurient travelers cleared the table before the host sat.  饥饿的旅客在主人坐下前就吃光了满桌菜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gluttonous</td><td>/ˈɡlʌtənəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贪吃的，暴食的</td><td>gluttonous habits<br>暴食的习惯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The emperor's gluttonous feasts lasted for several days.  皇帝暴食的宴席持续了好几天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 swinish</del></td><td>/ˈswaɪnɪʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贪吃的，粗鄙如猪的</td><td>swinish greed<br>猪一般的贪吃</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His swinish manners at table disgusted the other diners.  他餐桌上粗鄙的吃相让同桌人反感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 acquisitive</td><td>/əˈkwɪzətɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贪婪的</td><td>an acquisitive society<br>物欲横流的社会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The acquisitive collector bought every painting that came onto the market.  这位贪得无厌的收藏家买下了市面上出现的每一幅画。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 avid</td><td>/ˈævɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>急切渴望的，热衷的</td><td>an avid reader<br>热衷阅读的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My grandmother is an avid gardener even in winter.  我祖母即使在冬天也是个热衷园艺的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 covetous</td><td>/ˈkʌvətəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贪婪的，渴求财富的</td><td>covetous of his wealth<br>贪求他的财富</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He cast a covetous eye on his neighbor's newly bought tractor.  他贪婪地盯着邻居新买的拖拉机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grasping</td><td>/ˈɡræspɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>贪婪的，唯利是图的</td><td>a grasping moneylender<br>唯利是图的放贷人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His grasping relatives fought over the estate before the funeral ended.  他贪婪的亲戚在葬礼结束前就争夺遗产。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 greedy</td><td>/ˈɡriːdi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贪婪的，贪心的</td><td>greedy for money<br>贪财</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He grew greedy and lost everything in one bad deal.  他变得贪心，在一笔糟糕的交易中失去了一切。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hungry</td><td>/ˈhʌŋɡri/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>饥饿的，渴求的</td><td>hungry for praise<br>渴求赞扬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The workers came back hungry after ten hours outside.  工人们在外干了十小时，回来时饥肠辘辘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rapacious</td><td>/rəˈpeɪʃəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过度贪婪的</td><td>rapacious business practices<br>贪婪的商业行径</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rapacious business practices drove several small competitors out of the market.  贪婪的商业行径把几家小竞争者挤出了市场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ravenous</td><td>/ˈrævənəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>食量大的，贪食的；贪婪的</td><td>a ravenous appetite<br>极大的食量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After the long hike the boys had a ravenous appetite for anything.  长途徒步之后，男孩们对任何食物都胃口极大。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 thirsty</td><td>/ˈθɜːrsti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>渴望的；口渴的</td><td>thirsty for knowledge<br>渴求知识</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young reporter was thirsty for a really big story.  这位年轻记者渴望一条真正的大新闻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abstemious</td><td>/əbˈstiːmiəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（吃喝等）有节制的，节俭的</td><td>an abstemious diet<br>有节制的饮食</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He led an abstemious life, eating little and drinking less.  他生活节制，吃得少喝得更少。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 abstentious</del></td><td>/æbˈstɛnʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>节制的，有节欲的</td><td>an abstentious lifestyle<br>节制的生活方式</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monks led an abstentious life of prayer and labor.  僧侣们过着祈祷与劳作的节制生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 apathetic</td><td>/ˌæpəˈθetɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷漠的，无兴趣的</td><td>apathetic voters<br>冷漠的选民</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most students seemed apathetic about the election and did not vote.  大多数学生对这次选举漠不关心，也没有投票。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 indifferent</td><td>/ɪnˈdɪfrənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不感兴趣的，冷漠的；平庸的</td><td>indifferent to criticism<br>对批评漠不关心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is entirely indifferent to what her critics say.  她对批评者说什么完全无所谓。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 uneager</del></td><td>/ʌnˈiːɡər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不热切的，不情愿的</td><td>uneager to volunteer<br>不愿主动报名</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team was oddly uneager to take on the new project.  团队对接手新项目出奇地不积极。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unenthusiastic</td><td>/ˌʌnɪnθuːziˈæstɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不热情的，兴趣不大的</td><td>an unenthusiastic response<br>反应冷淡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His unenthusiastic nod told us he disliked the idea.  他不情不愿的点头说明他不喜欢这个主意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>votary</b></del></td><td>/ˈvoʊtəri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>崇拜者，信徒</td><td>a votary of art<br>艺术的崇拜者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A lifelong votary of modern art, she visited the museum weekly.  作为现代艺术的终身崇拜者，她每周都去博物馆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 acolyte</td><td>/ˈækəˌlaɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>助手，随从；信徒</td><td>a loyal acolyte<br>忠诚的追随者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The senator arrived with three acolytes carrying his papers.  参议员带着三名随从进场，他们拿着他的文件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adherent</td><td>/ədˈhɪrənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>追随者，拥护者</td><td>a devoted adherent<br>忠实的拥护者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The movement attracted thousands of adherents in a single year.  这场运动一年内就吸引了数千名拥护者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 devotee</td><td>/ˌdevəˈtiː/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>爱好者，热爱者</td><td>a devotee of opera<br>歌剧爱好者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 As a devotee of jazz, he attends every festival in the city.  作为爵士乐迷，他参加城里的每一个音乐节。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disciple</td><td>/dɪˈsaɪpl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>信徒，门徒，追随者</td><td>a devoted disciple<br>忠诚的信徒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He became a devoted disciple of the old master painter.  他成了这位老画家的忠实追随者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fan</td><td>/ˈfæn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>狂热爱好者，迷</td><td>a devoted fan<br>忠实的爱好者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thousands of fans waited outside the stadium all night.  数千名球迷在球场外等了整夜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fanatic</td><td>/fəˈnætɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>狂热者</td><td>a fitness fanatic<br>健身狂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My roommate is a fitness fanatic who trains twice a day.  我室友是个健身狂，一天训练两次。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 partisan</td><td>/ˈpɑːrtəzn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>党派偏见的；盲目拥护者</td><td>partisan politics<br>党派偏见的政治</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The debate quickly sank into partisan shouting about very old grievances.  辩论很快沦为带党派偏见的叫嚷，纠缠陈年旧怨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pupil</td><td>/ˈpjuːpəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>学生，弟子</td><td>a promising pupil<br>很有前途的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was her most promising pupil that difficult year.  在那个艰难的年份他是她最有前途的学生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 zealot</td><td>/ˈzelət/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>狂热者，极端主义者</td><td>a religious zealot<br>宗教狂热者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only a zealot would defend that policy so fiercely.  只有狂热者才会如此激烈地为那项政策辩护。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bellwether</td><td>/ˈbelweðər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>领导者，带头人</td><td>a bellwether of change<br>变化的风向标</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This small town has long been a bellwether of national opinion.  这个小镇长期以来是全国民意的风向标。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 leader</td><td>/ˈliːdər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>领导者，带头人</td><td>an industry leader<br>行业领军者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She became the leader of the movement while still a student.  她还是学生时就成了这场运动的领导者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>vouch</b></td><td>/vaʊtʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>担保，声称为真</td><td>vouch for his honesty<br>为他的诚实担保</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two former employers agreed to vouch for his honesty and skill.  两位前雇主同意为他的诚实和能力担保。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 attest</td><td>/əˈtest/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证实，为…作证</td><td>attest to his good character<br>证明他品行良好</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several neighbors will attest to his good character in court.  几位邻居将在法庭上证明他品行良好。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 avouch</del></td><td>/əˈvaʊtʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>断言，保证，证实</td><td>avouch the truth of it<br>保证其真实性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two witnesses avouched the accuracy of the merchant's account.  两名证人证实了这位商人陈述的准确性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 guarantee</td><td>/ˌɡerənˈtiː/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>保证，担保</td><td>guarantee the quality<br>保证质量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The maker guarantees every part for three full years.  厂商为每个部件提供整三年保证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 testify</td><td>/ˈtestəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>作证，证明</td><td>testify in court<br>在法庭上作证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two neighbors agreed to testify about the noise.  两位邻居同意就噪音一事作证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 warrant</td><td>/ˈwɔːrənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>保证，担保；使有正当理由</td><td>warrant the quality<br>保证质量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The factory warrants the quality of every tool it sells abroad.  这家工厂为其出口的每件工具的质量提供保证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 6

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>vulgar</b></td><td>/ˈvʌlɡər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗俗的，无教养的</td><td>vulgar display of wealth<br>粗俗的炫富</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their vulgar display of wealth embarrassed even their closest friends.  他们粗俗的炫富让连最亲近的朋友都感到难堪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bawdy</td><td>/ˈbɔːdi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>淫猥的，下流的</td><td>a bawdy song<br>一首下流的歌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sailors sang bawdy songs late into the night.  水手们唱着下流的歌唱到深夜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 coarse</td><td>/kɔːrs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗糙的，表面不平整的；粗俗的</td><td>coarse language<br>粗俗的言语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The workers used coarse language that shocked the visiting students.  工人们说的粗话让来访的学生们大吃一惊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crass</td><td>/kræs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗俗的，愚钝的</td><td>crass commercialism<br>赤裸裸的商业气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His crass joke silenced the entire table at dinner.  他粗俗的玩笑让整桌人都沉默了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crude</td><td>/ˈkruːd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗糙的，未加工的；粗俗的</td><td>crude oil<br>原油</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The furniture was crude but surprisingly sturdy and very cheap.  这家具做得粗糙，却意外结实又便宜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dirty</td><td>/ˈdɜːrti/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>肮脏的；卑鄙的</td><td>dirty windows<br>脏污的窗子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children came home with dirty hands and happy faces.  孩子们回家时手很脏，脸上却很开心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 filthy</td><td>/ˈfɪlθi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>污秽的，极脏的</td><td>filthy old clothes<br>污秽的旧衣服</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The kitchen floor was filthy after the long noisy party.  漫长喧闹的聚会后厨房地板脏得一塌糊涂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gross</td><td>/ˈɡroʊs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过于肥胖的；粗俗的</td><td>a gross, bloated figure<br>臃肿肥胖的身形</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The caricature gave the mayor a gross, swollen belly.  那幅漫画给市长画了个臃肿肥大的肚子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 lowbred</del></td><td>/ˈloʊˌbrɛd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗俗的，没教养的</td><td>lowbred manners<br>没教养的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hostess found his loud jokes lowbred and tiresome.  女主人觉得他大声的玩笑粗俗又乏味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 indecent</td><td>/ɪnˈdiːsnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不得体的，下流的</td><td>indecent language in public<br>公开场合的下流言语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The comedian was fined for using indecent language on live television.  这位喜剧演员因在直播电视上使用下流言语被罚款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nasty</td><td>/ˈnæsti/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人厌恶的，肮脏的；恶劣的</td><td>a nasty smell<br>难闻的气味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A nasty stain spread slowly across the pale carpet.  一块难看的污渍在浅色地毯上慢慢蔓延。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obscene</td><td>/ɑbˈsiːn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>淫秽的，猥亵的</td><td>obscene language<br>淫秽的言语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The magazine was banned for printing obscene photographs.  这本杂志因刊登淫秽照片而被查禁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ribald</td><td>/ˈrɪbəld/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（举止、言语）下流粗俗的</td><td>ribald jokes<br>粗俗下流的笑话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old sailor entertained the crew with ribald songs late into the night.  老水手用粗俗下流的歌曲让船员们乐到深夜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rude</td><td>/ˈruːd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗鲁的，无礼的</td><td>a rude remark<br>无礼的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was rude of him to leave without thanking the host.  他不谢主人就离开，很没礼貌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uncouth</td><td>/ʌnˈkuːθ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗鲁的，无教养的</td><td>uncouth table manners<br>粗野的餐桌举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His uncouth remarks at dinner offended nearly everyone at the table.  他在晚餐上粗鲁的言辞几乎惹恼了同桌所有人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 civilized</td><td>/ˈsɪvəˌlaɪzd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>文明的，有教养的</td><td>civilized behavior<br>文明的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They settled the dispute in a calm, civilized manner over tea.  他们喝茶时以平静文明的方式解决了争端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cultivated</td><td>/ˈkʌltɪveɪtɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有教养的，有修养的</td><td>a cultivated taste in art<br>高雅的艺术品位</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her cultivated manners impressed everyone at the dinner.  她优雅的举止让晚宴上的每个人都印象深刻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cultured</td><td>/ˈkʌltʃərd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有教养的，有修养的</td><td>a cultured gentleman<br>有教养的绅士</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was a cultured man who read poetry in four languages.  他是个有教养的人，能读四种语言的诗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 genteel</td><td>/dʒenˈtiːl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有教养的，不粗俗的</td><td>genteel manners<br>有教养的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her genteel accent betrayed an expensive and very traditional upbringing.  她文雅的口音透露出昂贵而传统的成长背景。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 polished</td><td>/ˈpɑːlɪʃt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有教养的</td><td>polished manners<br>优雅得体的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His polished manners impressed everyone at the formal dinner.  他优雅得体的举止让正式晚宴上的每个人都印象深刻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 refined</td><td>/rəˈfaɪnd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>精炼的，纯净的；有教养的</td><td>refined sugar<br>精制糖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Refined sugar has been stripped of every impurity.  精制糖已去除了所有杂质。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 urbane</td><td>/ərˈbeɪn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>彬彬有礼的，文雅的</td><td>an urbane host<br>文雅的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our urbane host guided the conversation with easy wit and grace.  我们文雅的主人以轻松的机智和风度引导着谈话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 well-bred</td><td>/wɛl ˈbrɛd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有教养的，有礼貌的</td><td>a well-bred young woman<br>一位有教养的年轻女子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The well-bred guest thanked the host before leaving.  这位有教养的客人离开前向主人道了谢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>vulnerable</b></td><td>/ˈvʌlnərəbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易受攻击的，脆弱的</td><td>vulnerable to attack<br>易受攻击</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wooden houses on the ridge are especially vulnerable to summer fires.  山脊上的木屋在夏季尤其容易受火灾侵袭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 assailable</del></td><td>/əˈseɪləbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易受攻击的，站不住脚的</td><td>an assailable claim<br>站不住脚的主张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His argument proved assailable on almost every point.  他的论证几乎每一点都站不住脚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 endangered</td><td>/ɪnˈdeɪndʒərd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>处于危险中的，濒危的</td><td>an endangered species<br>濒危物种</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rising temperatures have left many mountain bird species endangered.  气温上升使许多山地鸟类濒临危险。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exposed</td><td>/ɪkˈspoʊzd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无遮蔽的，易受攻击的</td><td>exposed to attack<br>易受攻击</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The camp sat on an exposed ridge with no shelter from wind.  营地位于毫无避风处的裸露山脊上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 subject</td><td>/ˈsʌbdʒekt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>取决于（其他因素）的，受制于</td><td>subject to approval<br>须经批准</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The entire plan is subject to approval by the city council.  整个方案须经市议会批准。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 susceptible</td><td>/səˈseptəbl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易受影响的；易感染的</td><td>susceptible to infection<br>易受感染</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young children are especially susceptible to infection during the cold months.  幼儿在寒冷的月份里尤其容易受到感染。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 guarded</td><td>/ˈɡɑːrdəd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>谨慎的，有保留的</td><td>a guarded response<br>有保留的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The minister gave a guarded welcome to the new proposal.  部长对新提案表示了有保留的欢迎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 protected</td><td>/prəˈtektɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>受保护的，有防护的</td><td>a protected species<br>受保护的物种</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nesting birds are protected by law in this park.  这个公园里筑巢的鸟受法律保护。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 shielded</td><td>/ˈʃiːldɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>受庇护的，被遮护的</td><td>shielded from criticism<br>免受批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The heir was shielded from every hardship throughout his childhood.  这位继承人整个童年都被保护得远离一切艰辛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>waddle</b></td><td>/ˈwɑːdl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>摇摇摆摆地走</td><td>waddle like a duck<br>像鸭子般摇摆走</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fat puppy waddled across the kitchen toward its water bowl.  那只胖胖的小狗摇摇摆摆地穿过厨房走向水碗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 careen</td><td>/kəˈriːn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>摇晃疾行，横冲直撞</td><td>careen down the hill<br>摇晃着冲下山坡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The empty cart careened down the hill and struck a fence.  空车摇晃着冲下山坡，撞上了篱笆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dodder</td><td>/ˈdɑːdər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>蹒跚，颤巍巍地行进</td><td>dodder along the path<br>沿小路蹒跚而行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old man doddered along the path with a cane in each hand.  老人两手各持一杖，沿小路蹒跚而行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reel</td><td>/riːl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>感到眩晕</td><td>reel from the blow<br>被打得头昏眼花</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The boxer reeled from the blow and clutched at the ropes.  拳手被那一击打得头昏眼花，抓住了围绳。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 teeter</td><td>/ˈtiːtər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>摇晃，摇摇欲坠</td><td>teeter on the edge<br>在边缘摇摇欲坠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company teetered on the edge of bankruptcy for several months.  公司数月来一直在破产边缘摇摇欲坠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 totter</td><td>/ˈtɑːtər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>摇摇晃晃地走，蹒跚</td><td>totter across the room<br>摇晃着走过房间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The toddler tottered a few steps and then sat down.  幼儿摇摇晃晃走了几步就坐下了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>waffle</b></td><td>/ˈwɑːfl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>含糊其辞，支吾</td><td>waffle about the plan<br>就计划含糊其辞</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The candidate waffled about the tax plan for a full ten minutes.  这位候选人就税收方案含糊其辞地扯了整整十分钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 babble</td><td>/ˈbæbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>发出含糊无意义的嘟囔声，胡乱说</td><td>babble on about nothing<br>没完没了地瞎扯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The toddler would babble happily for hours in his crib.  这个学步的孩子会在婴儿床里开心地咿呀乱说几个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blather</td><td>/ˈblæðər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>唠唠叨叨地说，胡扯</td><td>blather on about nothing<br>东拉西扯地唠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He blathered on about traffic while everyone waited to order.  大家等着点菜，他还在唠唠叨叨说交通。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 drivel</td><td>/ˈdrɪvl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>胡言乱语</td><td>talk complete drivel<br>满口胡言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pamphlet talked complete drivel about curing serious illness with colored light.  那本小册子满口胡言，说用彩色光能治重病。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gabble</td><td>/ˈɡæbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>急促而含糊地说</td><td>gabble away nervously<br>紧张地急促乱说</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The child gabbled excitedly about everything he had seen that day.  孩子兴奋地急促讲述那天见到的一切。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prattle</td><td>/ˈprætəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>闲扯，唠叨</td><td>prattle on for hours<br>唠叨好几个小时</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She prattled happily about her garden while we drank our tea.  我们喝茶时她开心地闲扯她的花园。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 articulate</td><td>/ɑːrˈtɪkjuleɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>清晰地表达；表达清晰的</td><td>articulate her concerns<br>清晰表达她的顾虑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She managed to articulate her concerns clearly at the board meeting.  她在董事会上清晰地表达了自己的顾虑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>waft</b></td><td>/wɑːft/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>飘荡，漂浮</td><td>waft through the air<br>在空中飘荡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The smell of fresh bread wafted through the open kitchen window.  新鲜面包的香味从敞开的厨房窗户飘荡进来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 buoy</td><td>/ˈbuːi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使充满勇气和力量，使振作</td><td>buoy his spirits<br>使他振作起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Kind letters from strangers buoyed her spirits during the long illness.  陌生人的暖心来信在她久病期间让她振作起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 drift</td><td>/ˈdrɪft/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>漂流，漫无目的地移动</td><td>drift from town to town<br>从一个城镇漂到另一个</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He drifted through Europe for two years without any plans.  他毫无计划地在欧洲漂了两年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 glide</td><td>/ɡlaɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>轻松地行动，轻松地通过</td><td>glide across the floor<br>轻盈地滑过地板</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The skaters glided across the frozen pond without making a single sound.  滑冰者们悄无声息地轻盈滑过结冰的池塘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hang</td><td>/ˈhæŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>悬挂；（烟雾、气味）弥漫不散</td><td>hang in the air<br>弥漫在空气中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A smell of jasmine hung in the warm evening air.  茉莉的香味弥漫在温暖的夜风里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hover</td><td>/ˈhʌvər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>盘旋，悬停；徘徊</td><td>hover in the air<br>在空中悬停</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A hummingbird hovered in front of the red feeder for several seconds.  一只蜂鸟在红色喂食器前悬停了几秒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 raft</td><td>/ˈræft/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>木筏；一大批</td><td>a raft of proposals<br>一大批提案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor announced a raft of new measures to reduce traffic.  市长宣布了一大批缓解交通的新措施。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 flounder</td><td>/ˈflaʊndər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>笨拙地行动，挣扎</td><td>flounder in the water<br>在水中挣扎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The swimmer floundered in the cold water before help arrived.  救援到来之前，游泳者在冷水中挣扎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sink</td><td>/ˈsɪŋk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>下沉，下降；水槽</td><td>sink below the horizon<br>沉到地平线下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old boat began to sink soon after the storm.  暴风雨过后不久那条旧船开始下沉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>wag</b></td><td>/wæɡ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>摇摆；摇动（尾巴）</td><td>wag its tail<br>摇尾巴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old dog wagged its tail whenever the children came home.  孩子们回家时，那只老狗总会摇尾巴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 swish</td><td>/ˈswɪʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>嗖嗖地挥动，摆动</td><td>swish its tail<br>甩动尾巴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The horse swished its tail to drive the flies away.  马甩动尾巴驱赶苍蝇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 switch</td><td>/ˈswɪtʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>迅速摆动，抽打；转换</td><td>switch its tail sharply<br>猛地甩尾</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cat switched its tail before pouncing on the toy.  猫在扑向玩具前猛甩尾巴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 waggle</td><td>/ˈwæɡəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>摇摆，摆动</td><td>waggle his eyebrows<br>挑动眉毛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He waggled his eyebrows to show that he was only joking.  他挑动眉毛表示自己只是在开玩笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>wan</b></td><td>/wɑːn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>苍白的，病态的</td><td>a wan smile<br>苍白无力的一笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave us a wan smile and went back to bed.  她苍白无力地笑了一下，又回床上躺着了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ashen</td><td>/ˈæʃən/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>灰白的，苍白的</td><td>an ashen face<br>苍白的脸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He came back from the hospital with an ashen face.  他从医院回来时脸色灰白。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ashy</td><td>/ˈæʃi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>灰白的，如灰般苍白的</td><td>ashy pale skin<br>灰白的皮肤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her ashy complexion alarmed everyone at the table.  她灰白的脸色让餐桌上的人都吃了一惊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blanched</td><td>/blæntʃt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>变白的，发白的</td><td>a blanched face<br>发白的脸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her blanched face showed how frightened she really was.  她发白的脸显出她真的很害怕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 livid</td><td>/ˈlɪvɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>铁青的，青灰色的；暴怒的</td><td>livid with rage<br>气得脸色铁青</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her face turned livid when she finally heard the news.  听到消息时她的脸色变得铁青。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lurid</td><td>/ˈlʊrɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>骇人的；（色彩）刺目的</td><td>lurid details<br>骇人的细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The newspaper printed lurid details that upset the victim's family deeply.  报纸刊登了骇人的细节，令受害者家人深感不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sickly</td><td>/ˈsɪkli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>病态的，苍白虚弱的</td><td>a sickly pale face<br>病态苍白的脸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He had a sickly complexion after weeks shut indoors.  在室内闷了几周后他面色病态苍白。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pale</td><td>/ˈpeɪl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>苍白的；浅色的</td><td>turn pale with fear<br>吓得脸色发白</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She turned pale when she heard the bad news.  听到坏消息时她脸色发白。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pallid</td><td>/ˈpælɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无生气的，缺乏活力的</td><td>a pallid imitation<br>毫无生气的模仿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The remake was a pallid imitation of a far livelier original.  这部翻拍片是对一部活力十足的原作的毫无生气的模仿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 blooming</td><td>/ˈbluːmɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>红润的；盛开的</td><td>blooming with health<br>气色红润健康</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She returned from the coast blooming with color and energy.  她从海边回来，脸色红润、精神饱满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 florid</td><td>/ˈflɔːrɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>辞藻华丽的，花哨的</td><td>florid prose<br>辞藻华丽的散文</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The critic mocked the florid prose of the young novelist.  评论家嘲讽这位年轻小说家辞藻华丽的文风。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 flush</td><td>/flʌʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、不及物动词</td><td>充裕的，有钱的；冲洗</td><td>flush with money<br>钱很充裕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After the bonus he was flush with money for months.  拿到奖金后他有好几个月钱都很充裕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 full-blooded</td><td>/ˌfʊl ˈblʌdɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>强有力的，充满活力的；纯种的</td><td>a full-blooded attack<br>全力以赴的进攻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team launched a full-blooded attack in the second half.  球队在下半场发起了猛烈进攻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 glowing</td><td>/ˈɡloʊɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>发光的，炽热的</td><td>glowing embers<br>发光的余烬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Glowing coals gave the little room a warm orange light.  发光的炭火给小屋带来温暖的橙色光线。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 red</td><td>/ˈred/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>红润的，红色的</td><td>red cheeks<br>红润的双颊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cold wind left her cheeks red and glowing.  冷风让她的双颊红润有光。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rosy</td><td>/ˈroʊzi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>乐观的</td><td>paint a rosy picture<br>描绘乐观前景</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their report paints a rosy picture that ignores the falling export figures.  他们的报告描绘出乐观前景，却忽视了下滑的出口数据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 rubicund</del></td><td>/ˈruːbɪkənd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>红润的，健康的</td><td>a rubicund complexion<br>红润的面色</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His rubicund complexion suggested a long life spent outdoors.  他红润的气色显示他常年在户外生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ruddy</td><td>/ˈrʌdi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>红润的，泛红的</td><td>ruddy cheeks<br>红润的双颊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The farmer's ruddy face showed years of outdoor work.  这位农民红润的脸显示出多年的户外劳作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sanguine</td><td>/ˈsæŋɡwɪn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>乐观的，确信的</td><td>sanguine about the future<br>对未来乐观</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She remains sanguine about the harvest despite three weeks without rain.  尽管三周无雨，她对收成依然乐观。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>warble</b></td><td>/ˈwɔːrbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>柔和地唱歌</td><td>warble a soft tune<br>轻柔地唱首曲子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A thrush warbled softly in the hedge just after first light.  天刚亮，一只画眉在树篱中柔和地鸣唱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quaver</td><td>/ˈkweɪvər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>（声音）颤抖，发颤</td><td>quaver with emotion<br>因激动而发颤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her voice quavered as she read the letter aloud.  她朗读那封信时声音发颤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trill</td><td>/ˈtrɪl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>用颤音唱；鸟鸣</td><td>trill a high note<br>用颤音唱高音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A lark trilled somewhere above the quiet meadow.  一只云雀在静谧的草地上方啼鸣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 croon</td><td>/kruːn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>低声吟唱，轻声哼唱</td><td>croon a lullaby<br>低声唱摇篮曲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mother crooned an old lullaby until the baby fell asleep.  母亲低声哼着一首老摇篮曲，直到婴儿睡去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>wane</b></td><td>/weɪn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>减少，衰退</td><td>wax and wane<br>盈亏消长，兴衰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Public interest in the scandal waned within a month.  公众对这桩丑闻的兴趣一个月内就减退了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abate</td><td>/əˈbeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>减轻，减弱；减少</td><td>the storm gradually abated<br>风暴逐渐减弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fever finally abated after two days of medication.  服药两天后，烧终于退了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decline</td><td>/dɪˈklaɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>拒绝；下降，衰退</td><td>decline an invitation<br>谢绝邀请</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She politely declined the invitation to speak at the ceremony.  她礼貌地谢绝了在典礼上发言的邀请。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 diminish</td><td>/dɪˈmɪnɪʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）减少；轻视，贬低</td><td>diminish in importance<br>重要性下降</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His influence diminished steadily after he left the board.  他离开董事会后影响力持续减弱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dwindle</td><td>/ˈdwɪndl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>逐渐减少</td><td>dwindle to nothing<br>逐渐减少至无</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their savings dwindled during the months of unemployment.  失业的几个月里他们的积蓄逐渐减少。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ease</td><td>/ˈiːz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>减轻，缓和；容易</td><td>ease the pain<br>减轻疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The medicine eased her headache within minutes of the first dose.  这药在服下第一剂几分钟内就缓解了她的头痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ebb</td><td>/ˈeb/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>衰退，减弱；退潮</td><td>his strength ebbed away<br>他的体力渐渐衰退</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Public enthusiasm for the project ebbed over the winter.  公众对这个项目的热情在冬天渐渐消退。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fall</td><td>/ˈfɔːl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>下降，落下；秋天</td><td>a sharp fall in prices<br>价格急剧下跌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Share prices fell again after the disappointing earnings report.  令人失望的财报公布后股价再次下跌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lessen</td><td>/ˈlesən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>减少，减轻</td><td>lessen the risk<br>减少风险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Simple exercises lessen the pain in his stiff shoulder.  简单的锻炼减轻了他僵硬肩膀的疼痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lower</td><td>/ˈloʊər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>降低，减少；较低的</td><td>lower the temperature<br>降低温度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bank lowered interest rates twice in one year.  银行一年内两次降低利率。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 moderate</td><td>/ˈmɑːdəreɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>缓和，减轻；适度的</td><td>moderate one's demands<br>降低自己的要求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He agreed to moderate his tone during the debate.  他同意在辩论中缓和语气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recede</td><td>/rɪˈsiːd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>退去，消退，减弱</td><td>recede into the distance<br>渐渐远去消失</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The floodwaters slowly receded from the village streets.  洪水缓缓从村庄的街道退去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shrink</td><td>/ʃrɪŋk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>（在数量或价值方面）降低，减小</td><td>shrink in value<br>价值缩水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The value of the old coins began to shrink after the scandal.  丑闻之后，那些旧币的价值开始缩水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 subside</td><td>/səbˈsaɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>减弱，平息；下沉</td><td>the pain finally subsided<br>疼痛终于减轻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The noise subsided as soon as the teacher walked in.  老师一进来，喧闹声就平息了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 taper</td><td>/ˈteɪpər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>逐渐减少，减弱</td><td>taper off gradually<br>逐渐减弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The storm tapered off gradually as the evening wore on.  随着夜色渐深，暴风雨逐渐平息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 accumulate</td><td>/əˈkjuːmjəleɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词、及物动词</td><td>逐渐增长，积累</td><td>accumulate wealth<br>积累财富</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Dust had accumulated on the shelves during our absence.  我们离开期间架子上积了灰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 balloon</td><td>/bəˈluːn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>迅速增加</td><td>costs have ballooned<br>成本迅速膨胀</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Repair costs ballooned from two thousand to twenty thousand dollars.  维修费用从两千美元猛增到两万美元。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 burgeon</td><td>/ˈbɜːrdʒən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>迅速成长扩大，蓬勃发展</td><td>a burgeoning market<br>迅速发展的市场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city's tech sector burgeoned after the new university opened.  新大学开办后，该市的科技产业迅速壮大。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enlarge</td><td>/ɪnˈlɑːrdʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>扩大，增大</td><td>enlarge the photograph<br>放大照片</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They enlarged the kitchen by removing one interior wall.  他们拆掉一道内墙把厨房扩大了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 escalate</td><td>/ˈeskəleɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）（战争等）升级，扩大</td><td>escalate into violence<br>升级为暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The border dispute quickly escalated into a full-scale armed conflict.  边界争端迅速升级为全面武装冲突。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 expand</td><td>/ɪkˈspænd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>扩大，扩张；详述</td><td>expand the business<br>扩大业务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company plans to expand into three new markets.  公司计划扩张到三个新市场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 grow</td><td>/ˈɡroʊ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>种植，生长；成长</td><td>grow vegetables<br>种蔬菜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They grow tomatoes and beans on a small rooftop garden.  他们在小小的屋顶花园里种番茄和豆子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 increase</td><td>/ɪnˈkriːs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>增加，增长</td><td>increase production sharply<br>大幅提高产量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sales increased steadily after the store extended its opening hours.  延长营业时间后销量稳步增长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 intensify</td><td>/ɪnˈtensɪfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>加强，激化</td><td>intensify the pressure<br>加大压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rain intensified just as we reached the summit.  我们刚到山顶，雨就更大了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mushroom</td><td>/ˈmʌʃruːm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>蘑菇；迅速增长</td><td>mushroom into a crisis<br>迅速演变成危机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Small online shops mushroomed across the country within two years.  小型网店两年内在全国迅速涌现。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rise</td><td>/ˈraɪz/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>上升，兴起</td><td>the rise of an empire<br>一个帝国的兴起</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Historians still debate the rise of that short-lived empire.  历史学家仍在争论那个短命帝国的兴起。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ball</td><td>/ˈbɔːl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>球；球状物，一团</td><td>a pale ball of light<br>一团淡淡的光</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The moon hung like a pale ball above the rooftops.  月亮像一个苍白的圆球挂在屋顶上方。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 soar</td><td>/ˈsɔːr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>猛增，飞升</td><td>costs soar<br>成本猛增</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Building costs soared during the second year of construction.  施工第二年建筑成本猛增。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wax</td><td>/wæks/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>增大，增强</td><td>wax and wane<br>盛衰交替，此消彼长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His influence in the party waxed steadily during the long campaign.  在漫长的竞选中，他在党内的影响力稳步增强。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>wanting</b></td><td>/ˈwɑːntɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>未出现的，缺少的；未达到要求的</td><td>found wanting<br>被发现有欠缺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bridge was tested and found wanting in several important respects.  这座桥经过检测，在几个重要方面被发现有欠缺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 absent</td><td>/ˈæbsənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>缺席的，不存在的</td><td>largely absent from<br>在…中基本不存在</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Such customs are entirely absent from the northern villages.  这类习俗在北方村庄完全不存在。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lacking</td><td>/ˈlækɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>缺少的，不足的</td><td>lacking in confidence<br>缺乏自信</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plan was lacking in detail and had to be revised.  这个计划缺少细节，必须修改。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 missing</td><td>/ˈmɪsɪŋ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>缺失的，失踪的</td><td>the missing pages<br>缺失的页面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two pages are missing from the only surviving copy.  唯一留存的副本缺失了两页。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nonexistent</td><td>/ˌnɑːnɪɡˈzɪstənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不存在的</td><td>virtually nonexistent<br>几乎不存在的</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Public transport in that village is virtually nonexistent.  那个村子几乎没有公共交通。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deficient</td><td>/dɪˈfɪʃənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不足的，缺乏的</td><td>deficient in vitamins<br>缺乏维生素</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The diet was deficient in iron and essential vitamins.  这种饮食缺乏铁和必需的维生素。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 dissatisfactory</del></td><td>/ˌdɪsˌsætɪsˈfæktəri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人不满意的</td><td>a dissatisfactory outcome<br>令人不满的结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee called the report dissatisfactory and demanded revisions.  委员会称该报告不令人满意，要求修改。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ill</td><td>/ˈɪl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>祸害，弊病；生病的</td><td>social ills<br>社会弊病</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report lists the ills afflicting the health system.  报告列出了困扰医疗系统的种种弊病。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inferior</td><td>/ɪnˈfɪriər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>低等的，次等的</td><td>inferior in quality<br>质量较差</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Customers complained that the new fabric was clearly inferior.  顾客抱怨新面料明显质量较差。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lousy</td><td>/ˈlaʊzi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>多得很的，充满的；糟糕的</td><td>lousy with tourists<br>游客多得很</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 In August the town is lousy with tourists from every corner of Europe.  八月这座小镇挤满了来自欧洲各地的游客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 paltry</td><td>/ˈpɔːltri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无价值的；微不足道的</td><td>a paltry sum<br>微不足道的一笔钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They offered a paltry sum for a painting worth thousands of dollars.  他们为一幅价值数千美元的画只出了微不足道的一笔钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 poor</td><td>/pʊr/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>贫瘠的，贫乏的；贫穷的</td><td>poor rocky soil<br>贫瘠的石质土壤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The harvest was poor after a whole summer without rain.  整个夏天没下雨，收成很差。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unacceptable</td><td>/ˌʌnəkˈseptəbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可接受的</td><td>unacceptable behavior<br>不可接受的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Such rudeness to customers is completely unacceptable here.  对顾客如此无礼在这里完全不可接受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unsatisfactory</td><td>/ˌʌnsætɪsˈfæktəri/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不令人满意的</td><td>an unsatisfactory answer<br>不令人满意的答复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His unsatisfactory explanation only raised more questions.  他不令人满意的解释只带来了更多疑问。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wretched</td><td>/ˈretʃɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极差的；沮丧的</td><td>wretched living conditions<br>恶劣的居住条件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Families lived in wretched conditions beside the flooded riverbank.  一些家庭在泛滥的河岸边过着极差的生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 existent</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɪstənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>存在的，现存的</td><td>the existent records<br>现存的记录</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only two existent copies of that pamphlet remain in the archive.  档案馆里只剩那本小册子的两份现存副本。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 present</td><td>/ˈprezənt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>礼物；赠送</td><td>a birthday present<br>一份生日礼物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She wrapped the birthday present in bright blue paper.  她用亮蓝色的纸包好了生日礼物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 acceptable</td><td>/əkˈseptəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>可接受的，令人满意的</td><td>an acceptable compromise<br>可接受的妥协</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The revised offer seemed acceptable to both sides.  修改后的提议似乎双方都能接受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 adequate</td><td>/ˈædəkwət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>足够的，勉强合格的</td><td>adequate supplies<br>充足的供应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The room was small but adequate for two students.  房间不大，但对两名学生来说足够了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 passable</td><td>/ˈpæsəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可通行的；尚可的</td><td>a passable mountain road<br>可通行的山路</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The road is barely passable after the spring floods.  春季洪水过后，这条路勉强可以通行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tolerable</td><td>/ˈtɑːlərəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>可容忍的；尚可的</td><td>a tolerable level of noise<br>可容忍的噪音水平</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Conditions in the shelter were crowded but tolerable.  避难所里拥挤，但还能忍受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 7

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>warmonger</b></td><td>/ˈwɔːrmʌŋɡər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>好战者</td><td>branded a warmonger<br>被称为好战分子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Opposition papers branded the new minister a reckless warmonger.  反对派报纸把这位新部长称为轻率的好战分子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 belligerent</td><td>/bəˈlɪdʒərənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>好斗的，好战的</td><td>a belligerent tone<br>好斗的口气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He answered the question in a belligerent tone that shocked us.  他用一种令我们震惊的好斗口气回答了这个问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hawk</td><td>/ˈhɔːk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>叫卖，沿街推销</td><td>hawk cheap souvenirs<br>叫卖廉价纪念品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Vendors hawked cheap souvenirs outside the crowded temple gate.  小贩们在拥挤的庙门外叫卖廉价纪念品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 jingoist</del></td><td>/ˈdʒɪŋɡoʊɪst/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>极端爱国的好战分子</td><td>an outspoken jingoist<br>直言不讳的好战爱国者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The columnist was denounced as a jingoist eager for another foreign war.  这位专栏作家被谴责为渴望再打一场对外战争的好战爱国者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 war hawk</td><td>/wɔːr ˈhɔːk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>主战派，鹰派人物</td><td>a leading war hawk<br>主要的主战派人物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The senator built his career as an unapologetic war hawk.  这位参议员以毫不掩饰的鹰派立场建立起自己的事业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dove</td><td>/ˈdʌv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>主和派，鸽派；鸽子</td><td>hawks and doves<br>鹰派与鸽派</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cabinet split between hawks and doves over the treaty.  内阁就该条约分裂为鹰派与鸽派。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pacifist</td><td>/ˈpæsɪfɪst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>和平主义者；和平主义的</td><td>a pacifist movement<br>和平主义运动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 As a pacifist he refused to carry any weapon.  作为和平主义者，他拒绝携带任何武器。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>warp</b></td><td>/wɔːrp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>曲解</td><td>warp the truth<br>曲解真相</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A careless English translation had warped the author's original meaning.  一个粗心的英文译本曲解了作者的原意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 distort</td><td>/dɪˈstɔːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扭曲，歪曲</td><td>distort the facts<br>歪曲事实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Editing the tape that way would distort the facts of the interview.  那样剪辑录音会歪曲采访的事实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 falsify</td><td>/ˈfɔːlsəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>篡改，伪造；证明为假</td><td>falsify the records<br>篡改记录</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An accountant was accused of falsifying the company's records.  一名会计被指控篡改公司账目。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 misinterpret</td><td>/ˌmɪsɪnˈtɜːrprɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>误解，误读</td><td>misinterpret the data<br>误读数据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Readers often misinterpret irony as sincere praise.  读者常把讽刺误解为真诚的赞美。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 misrepresent</td><td>/ˌmɪsˌreprɪˈzent/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>误传，篡改</td><td>misrepresent the facts<br>歪曲事实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics accused the report of misrepresenting the facts to favor one side.  批评者指责这份报告歪曲事实以偏向一方。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 twist</td><td>/ˈtwɪst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>曲解，扭曲；拧</td><td>twist someone's words<br>曲解某人的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do not twist my words to suit your own argument.  不要为了你自己的论点曲解我的话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 clarify</td><td>/ˈklerɪfaɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阐明，澄清</td><td>clarify the rules<br>澄清规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dean clarified the rules after weeks of confused complaints.  在数周混乱的投诉后，院长澄清了规则。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 clear</td><td>/ˈklɪr/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明确的，确凿的；清楚的</td><td>a clear majority<br>明显的多数</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The party won by a clear majority of votes.  该党以明显多数票获胜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 explain</td><td>/ɪkˈspleɪn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解释，说明</td><td>explain the reason<br>解释原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Could you explain why the experiment failed the second time?  你能解释一下第二次实验为何失败吗？</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 illuminate</td><td>/ɪˈluːmɪneɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阐明</td><td>illuminate the problem<br>阐明问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her lecture illuminated a problem that had puzzled economists for years.  她的讲座阐明了一个困扰经济学家多年的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 illustrate</td><td>/ˈɪləsˌtreɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>说明，举例说明</td><td>illustrate the point<br>说明这一点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 These figures illustrate how quickly the population has grown.  这些数字说明人口增长得多么迅速。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>warrant</b></td><td>/ˈwɔːrənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>保证，担保；使有正当理由</td><td>warrant the quality<br>保证质量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The factory warrants the quality of every tool it sells abroad.  这家工厂为其出口的每件工具的质量提供保证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 approbate</del></td><td>/ˈæprəbeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞许，认可</td><td>approbate the decision<br>认可这一决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board approbated the merger after a long private debate.  经过长时间闭门辩论，董事会认可了这次合并。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 authorize</td><td>/ˈɔːθəˌraɪz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>授权，批准，委托</td><td>authorize the payment<br>批准这笔付款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only the director can authorize spending above that limit.  只有主管能批准超出该限额的开支。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clear</td><td>/ˈklɪr/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明确的，确凿的；清楚的</td><td>a clear majority<br>明显的多数</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The party won by a clear majority of votes.  该党以明显多数票获胜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 confirm</td><td>/kənˈfɜːrm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证实，确认，进一步确定</td><td>confirm the findings<br>证实这些发现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Later tests confirmed what the first team had suspected.  后来的检测证实了第一个团队的猜测。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 finalize</td><td>/ˈfaɪnəˌlaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>最终确定，敲定</td><td>finalize the agreement<br>敲定协议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Both sides hope to finalize the agreement before December.  双方希望在十二月前敲定协议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 formalize</td><td>/ˈfɔːrməˌlaɪz/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使正式化，使规范化</td><td>formalize the agreement<br>使协议正式化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two firms decided to formalize their loose partnership.  两家公司决定把松散的合作正式化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ratify</td><td>/ˈrætɪfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（官方地）认可，批准</td><td>ratify the peace treaty<br>批准和平条约</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The senate voted to ratify the peace treaty after weeks of debate.  经过数周辩论，参议院投票批准了和平条约。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sanction</td><td>/ˈsæŋkʃn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>批准，同意，认可</td><td>sanction the new policy<br>批准这项新政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board refused to sanction the plan without a detailed budget.  董事会拒绝在没有详细预算的情况下批准该计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 decline</td><td>/dɪˈklaɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>拒绝；下降，衰退</td><td>decline an invitation<br>谢绝邀请</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She politely declined the invitation to speak at the ceremony.  她礼貌地谢绝了在典礼上发言的邀请。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deny</td><td>/dɪˈnaɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认；拒绝给予</td><td>flatly deny the charge<br>断然否认指控</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I do not deny that the plan carries some very real risks.  我不否认这个计划确实带有一些实实在在的风险。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disallow</td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈlaʊ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>不许，驳回</td><td>disallow the goal<br>判进球无效</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The referee disallowed the goal for an obvious offside.  裁判因明显越位判该进球无效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disapprove</td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈpruːv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>不赞成，反对</td><td>disapprove of the plan<br>不赞成该计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her parents strongly disapprove of the plan to drop out.  她父母强烈不赞成退学的计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 negative</td><td>/ˈneɡətɪv/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>否决，反驳；负面的</td><td>negative the proposal<br>否决这项提案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee negatived the motion after a short debate.  委员会经短暂辩论后否决了该动议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reject</td><td>/rɪˈdʒekt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>拒绝，驳回；废品</td><td>reject the proposal<br>否决提案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee voted to reject the proposal without further discussion.  委员会投票否决了该提案，不再讨论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 veto</td><td>/ˈviːtoʊ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>否决，禁止</td><td>veto the proposed bill<br>否决拟议的法案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The governor promised to veto the bill if it reached his desk.  州长承诺，若这项法案送到他手上就予以否决。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>wary</b></td><td>/ˈweri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>警惕的，谨慎的</td><td>wary of strangers<br>对陌生人警惕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Children are taught to be wary of strangers offering free rides.  孩子们被教导要警惕主动提供搭车的陌生人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 alert</td><td>/əˈlɜːrt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>警觉的，机敏的；警报</td><td>stay alert<br>保持警觉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Night guards must stay alert even during the quietest hours.  夜间守卫即便在最安静的时段也必须保持警觉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cautious</td><td>/ˈkɔːʃəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>谨慎的，小心的</td><td>a cautious approach<br>谨慎的做法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Investors remained cautious until the election results were announced.  在选举结果公布前投资者一直很谨慎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chary</td><td>/ˈtʃeri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非常谨慎的</td><td>chary of praise<br>不轻易给出赞美</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Editors are chary of publishing claims that cannot be checked.  编辑对发表无法核实的说法非常谨慎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 circumspect</td><td>/ˈsɜːrkəmspekt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>谨慎的，小心的</td><td>remain circumspect in public<br>在公开场合保持谨慎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Diplomats stay circumspect in public and blunt behind closed doors.  外交官在公开场合谨慎，关起门来则直言不讳。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conservative</td><td>/kənˈsɜːrvətɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>守旧的，不愿改变的；不招摇的，低调的</td><td>a conservative estimate<br>保守的估计</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her parents were too conservative to accept such an unconventional career choice.  她的父母太守旧，无法接受这样不合常规的职业选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 guarded</td><td>/ˈɡɑːrdəd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>谨慎的，有保留的</td><td>a guarded response<br>有保留的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The minister gave a guarded welcome to the new proposal.  部长对新提案表示了有保留的欢迎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 heedful</del></td><td>/ˈhiːdfəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>留心的，注意的</td><td>heedful of the warning<br>留意警告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heedful of the forecast, the climbers turned back early.  留意到天气预报，登山者提早返回了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vigilant</td><td>/ˈvɪdʒɪlənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>警醒的，警惕的</td><td>remain vigilant<br>保持警惕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Guards must remain vigilant throughout the long and quiet night shift.  守卫在漫长安静的夜班中必须始终保持警惕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 watchful</td><td>/ˈwɑːtʃfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>警惕的，注意的</td><td>a watchful eye<br>警惕的目光</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children played under their grandmother's watchful eye.  孩子们在祖母警惕的注视下玩耍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 careless</td><td>/ˈkerləs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗心的，疏忽的</td><td>a careless mistake<br>粗心的错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One careless remark ruined months of patient negotiation.  一句不慎的话毁了数月的耐心谈判。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 heedless</td><td>/ˈhiːdləs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不注意的，不顾的</td><td>heedless of danger<br>不顾危险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He ran into the street heedless of the traffic.  他不顾车流冲进了街道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 incautious</td><td>/ɪnˈkɔʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不谨慎的，轻率的</td><td>an incautious promise<br>轻率的承诺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His incautious driving nearly caused a serious accident yesterday.  他不谨慎的驾驶昨天差点造成严重事故。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unguarded</td><td>/ˌʌnˈɡɑːrdɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无防备的，不谨慎的</td><td>an unguarded moment<br>毫无防备的一刻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 In an unguarded moment he revealed the whole plan.  在毫无防备的一刻他说出了整个计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unmindful</td><td>/ʌnˈmaɪndfəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不留意的，未察觉的</td><td>unmindful of danger<br>不顾危险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Unmindful of the time, they talked until well past midnight.  他们没在意时间，一直聊到深夜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unwary</td><td>/ʌnˈweri/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不警惕的，粗心的</td><td>trap the unwary traveler<br>诱骗粗心的旅客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Such websites are designed to trick the unwary buyer.  这类网站专门欺骗不加防范的买家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>wastrel</b></td><td>/ˈweɪstrəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>肆意挥霍的人，败家子</td><td>a drunken wastrel<br>酗酒的败家子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The family fortune was spent by a wastrel who gambled nightly.  家族财产被一个夜夜赌博的败家子花光了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 fritterer</del></td><td>/ˈfrɪtərər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>浪费时间或金钱的人</td><td>a hopeless fritterer<br>无可救药的挥霍者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her brother is a fritterer who wastes both his savings and his talent.  她弟弟是个挥霍者，既浪费积蓄也浪费才华。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
