<tr><td>↳反 blissful</td><td>/ˈblɪsfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极幸福的，快乐的</td><td>a blissful silence<br>幸福的宁静</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They spent a blissful week beside the quiet mountain lake.  他们在宁静的山湖边度过了幸福的一周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 buoyant</td><td>/ˈbuːjənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>心情好的</td><td>in a buoyant mood<br>心情愉快</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He arrived in a buoyant mood after hearing the good news.  听到好消息后，他心情愉快地到了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cheerful</td><td>/ˈtʃɪrfəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>快乐的，愉快的</td><td>a cheerful greeting<br>愉快的问候</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stayed cheerful even after the long delay.  即使长时间延误后他仍保持愉快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 delighted</td><td>/dɪˈlaɪtəd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>高兴的，欣喜的</td><td>delighted with the gift<br>对礼物很欣喜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was delighted to hear about her friend's promotion.  听说朋友升职，她非常高兴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 glad</td><td>/ˈɡlæd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>高兴的，乐意的</td><td>glad to help<br>乐意帮忙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I am always glad to help a neighbor in trouble.  我总是乐意帮助有困难的邻居。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 happy</td><td>/ˈhæpi/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>快乐的，幸福的</td><td>a happy ending<br>幸福的结局</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children were happy to see snow for the first time.  孩子们第一次见到雪很开心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 joyful</td><td>/ˈdʒɔɪfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>欢乐的，喜悦的</td><td>a joyful reunion<br>欢乐的团聚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The village held a joyful celebration after the harvest.  收获后村里举行了欢乐的庆祝。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 jubilant</td><td>/ˈdʒuːbɪlənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>喜悦的</td><td>a jubilant crowd<br>欢欣的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nominee gave a short speech before the jubilant crowd.  被提名人在欢欣鼓舞的人群前发表了简短讲话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>wry</b></td><td>/raɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（笑容）苦涩讽刺的；坚持错误的</td><td>a wry smile<br>苦笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave a wry smile and admitted his own mistake.  他苦笑一下，承认了自己的错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 headstrong</td><td>/ˈhedˌstrɔːŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>任性的，固执的</td><td>a headstrong child<br>任性的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The headstrong young man ignored every warning from his parents.  这个任性的年轻人对父母的每句劝告都置之不理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obstinate</td><td>/ˈɑːbstɪnət/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的</td><td>an obstinate refusal<br>固执的拒绝</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her obstinate refusal to compromise delayed the agreement by several months.  她固执地拒绝妥协，使协议推迟了几个月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 pertinacious</del></td><td>/ˌpɜːrtnˈeɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>顽固的，固执地坚持的</td><td>pertinacious in her demands<br>固执地坚持要求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pertinacious reporter kept calling until she got an answer.  这位执着的记者不停打电话，直到得到答复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perverse</td><td>/pərˈvɜːrs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>反常固执的，任性的</td><td>take perverse pleasure<br>反常地感到快意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He takes perverse pleasure in arguing against every popular opinion.  他反常地乐于反驳每一个流行观点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stubborn</td><td>/ˈstʌbərn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的，顽固的</td><td>as stubborn as a mule<br>像骡子一样固执</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My grandfather was too stubborn to see a doctor.  我祖父太固执，不肯去看医生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wrongheaded</td><td>/ˌrɔːŋˈhedɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执错误的，判断有误的</td><td>a wrongheaded policy<br>错误的政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Economists called the new tax a wrongheaded policy from the start.  经济学家从一开始就称这项新税是错误政策。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 amenable</td><td>/əˈmiːnəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，服从的</td><td>amenable to suggestion<br>愿意接受建议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new manager is amenable to any reasonable suggestion.  这位新经理对任何合理的建议都乐于接受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 compliant</td><td>/kəmˈplaɪənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的</td><td>a compliant employee<br>顺从的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children were unusually compliant during the long ceremony.  在漫长的仪式上，孩子们出奇地听话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 complying</td><td>/kəmˈplaɪɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，遵从的</td><td>complying with the rules<br>遵从规定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Firms complying with the standard receive a tax rebate.  遵从该标准的企业可获得退税。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 flexible</td><td>/ˈfleksəbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>灵活的，可变的；易受影响的</td><td>a flexible schedule<br>灵活的日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company offers a flexible schedule to parents of young children.  公司为有幼儿的员工提供灵活的日程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pliable</td><td>/ˈplaɪəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易弯曲的，柔软的；易受影响的，温顺的</td><td>pliable young minds<br>易受影响的年轻心智</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young minds are pliable and easily shaped by the adults around them.  年轻的心智易受影响，很容易被身边的成年人塑造。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pliant</td><td>/ˈplaɪənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易弯曲的；顺从的</td><td>a pliant assistant<br>顺从的助手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wanted a pliant assistant who would never question his decisions.  他想要一个顺从的助手，永远不会质疑他的决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 relenting</td><td>/rɪˈlentɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>态度软化的，让步的</td><td>show a relenting attitude<br>表现出软化的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After hours of pleading, his relenting expression gave us hope.  在几小时的哀求之后，他态度软化的表情给了我们希望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 yielding</td><td>/ˈjiːldɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，易屈服的</td><td>a yielding personality<br>顺从的性格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her yielding nature made her an easy target for pushy salesmen.  她顺从的性格使她很容易被强势的推销员盯上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 10

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>xenophobe</b></td><td>/ˈzenəfoʊb/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>仇外者；排外者</td><td>a raging xenophobe<br>极端的仇外者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The candidate's speech pleased no one except a few noisy xenophobes.  候选人的演讲除了几个喧闹的仇外者外，谁都不满意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 xenomania</del></td><td>/ˌzenoʊˈmeɪniə/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>媚外狂热，崇外</td><td>cultural xenomania<br>文化上的媚外</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The essay criticized the xenomania of the country's young elite.  文章批评了该国年轻精英的媚外倾向。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>yoke</b></td><td>/joʊk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使结合，联结；给…上轭</td><td>yoke two oxen together<br>把两头牛套在一起</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The farmer yoked two oxen together to pull the heavy plow.  农夫把两头牛套在一起拉那沉重的犁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chain</td><td>/ˈtʃeɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>连接，用链系住；链条</td><td>chain the parts together<br>把各部分连起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He chained his bicycle to the railing outside the shop.  他把自行车锁在店外的栏杆上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conjugate</td><td>/ˈkɑːndʒəɡeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>结合，配对；成对的</td><td>conjugate two molecules<br>使两个分子结合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers conjugate the drug to an antibody for precise delivery.  研究者把药物与抗体结合以实现精准投递。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hook</td><td>/ˈhʊk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>钩住，连接；钩子</td><td>hook the trailer on<br>把拖车挂上</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She hooked the two cables together before starting the engine.  她把两根电缆连接好才启动发动机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 interconnect</td><td>/ˌɪntərkəˈnekt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>互相连接</td><td>interconnect the networks<br>使各网络互连</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Bridges interconnect the islands so the ferry is no longer needed.  桥梁把各岛连接起来，渡船已无必要。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 join</td><td>/ˈdʒɔɪn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>连接，接合；加入</td><td>join the two pieces<br>把两块接起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Join the ends of the rope with a firm knot.  用牢固的结把绳子两端接起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 link</td><td>/ˈlɪŋk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>联系，纽带；连接</td><td>a close link<br>紧密的联系</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers found a close link between sleep and memory.  研究者发现睡眠与记忆之间存在紧密联系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unite</td><td>/juˈnaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>联合，团结</td><td>unite against a threat<br>联合抵御威胁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crisis united the villagers as nothing else had.  这场危机以前所未有的方式团结了村民。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disconnect</td><td>/dɪskəˈnekt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>断开，使分离</td><td>disconnect the power<br>切断电源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please disconnect the printer before you open its back panel.  打开后盖前请先断开打印机的连接。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 disjoin</del></td><td>/dɪsˈdʒɔɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使分开，使分离</td><td>disjoin the two parts<br>把两部分分开</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The engineer had to disjoin the pipes to replace a valve.  工程师得把管子分开才能更换阀门。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 dissever</del></td><td>/dɪˈsevər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>分开，切断</td><td>dissever the two halves<br>把两半分开</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The war dissevered families that had lived together for generations.  战争把世代同住的家庭分割开来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 disunite</del></td><td>/ˌdɪsjuˈnaɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使分裂，使不团结</td><td>disunite the party<br>使党派分裂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tax proposal disunited a coalition that had held for years.  这项税收提案使维持多年的联盟分裂了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 separate</td><td>/ˈsepəreɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>分开，分离</td><td>separate the yolks<br>把蛋黄分离出来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please separate the recyclable waste from the ordinary rubbish.  请把可回收垃圾与普通垃圾分开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sunder</td><td>/ˈsʌndər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>分开，割断</td><td>sunder the alliance<br>使联盟破裂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bitter dispute sundered an alliance that had lasted decades.  这场激烈的争端割断了维持数十年的联盟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unchain</td><td>/ʌnˈtʃeɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解开锁链，释放</td><td>unchain the dog<br>把狗解开链子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The keeper unchained the old elephant and let it wander freely.  饲养员解开老象的链子，让它自由走动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unlink</td><td>/ʌnˈlɪŋk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>断开，解除关联</td><td>unlink the two accounts<br>解除两账户关联</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 You can unlink your bank card from the app anytime.  你可随时解除银行卡与应用的关联。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unyoke</del></td><td>/ʌnˈjoʊk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解开轭；使分离，解脱</td><td>unyoke the oxen<br>给牛解轭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At dusk the workers unyoked the oxen and led them to water.  黄昏时工人给牛解开轭，牵它们去饮水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>yokel</b></td><td>/ˈjoʊkl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>乡下人；土包子</td><td>a gawking yokel<br>呆看的乡下人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stood in the marble lobby like a gawking country yokel.  他站在大理石大堂里，像个东张西望的乡下土包子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bucolic</td><td>/bjuˈkɑːlɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>乡村的，田园的</td><td>a bucolic landscape<br>田园风光</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They spent the summer in a bucolic village far from traffic.  他们在远离车流的田园村庄度过了夏天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bumpkin</td><td>/ˈbʌmpkɪn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>乡下人，土包子</td><td>a country bumpkin<br>乡下土包子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 In the city he felt like a bumpkin staring at the towers.  在城里他觉得自己像个盯着高楼看的乡下人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 churl</td><td>/tʃɜrl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>粗鄙的人，乡下佬</td><td>behave like a churl<br>举止像个粗鄙之人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only a churl would refuse to thank his host.  只有粗鄙之人才会不感谢主人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 provincial</td><td>/prəˈvɪnʃl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>狭隘（的）</td><td>provincial tastes<br>狭隘的品味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His provincial tastes made him suspicious of anything foreign or unfamiliar.  他狭隘的品味使他对一切外来或陌生的事物都怀疑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rustic</td><td>/ˈrʌstɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>乡下人，头脑简单的人；粗俗的</td><td>rustic manners<br>粗俗的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His rustic manners amused the guests at the elegant city dinner.  他粗俗的举止让城里雅致晚宴上的客人觉得好笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cosmopolitan</td><td>/ˌkɑːzməˈpɑːlɪtən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>有世界性眼光的，包容的</td><td>a cosmopolitan city<br>国际化的都市</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years abroad gave her a cosmopolitan outlook her old classmates clearly lacked.  多年国外生活给了她老同学明显缺乏的世界性眼光。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>zeal</b></td><td>/ziːl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>热心，热诚</td><td>religious zeal<br>宗教热情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She took up the campaign with the zeal of a true believer.  她以真正信徒的热诚投入了这场运动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ardor</td><td>/ˈɑːrdər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>狂热</td><td>revolutionary ardor<br>革命热情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young recruits sang with revolutionary ardor all the way to the front.  年轻的新兵一路满怀革命热情地高唱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 devotion</td><td>/dɪˈvoʊʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>忠诚，挚爱；献身</td><td>devotion to duty<br>忠于职守</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her devotion to her students earned her wide respect.  她对学生的挚爱赢得了广泛的尊敬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enthusiasm</td><td>/ɪnˈθuːziæzəm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>热情，热忱</td><td>greet the plan with enthusiasm<br>热情地迎接该计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her enthusiasm for the project convinced the whole committee.  她对这个项目的热情说服了整个委员会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 fervidness</del></td><td>/ˈfɜrvɪdnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>热烈，狂热</td><td>the fervidness of youth<br>青春的热烈</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fervidness of his belief unsettled his calmer colleagues.  他信念的狂热让较为冷静的同事不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fervor</td><td>/ˈfɜːrvər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>热情，热烈</td><td>patriotic fervor<br>爱国热情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crowd sang the anthem with surprising fervor.  人群以惊人的热情唱起国歌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 passion</td><td>/ˈpæʃən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>热情，激情</td><td>a passion for music<br>对音乐的热爱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He talks about old trains with genuine passion.  他谈起老火车时充满真挚的热情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 apathy</td><td>/ˈæpəθi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>缺乏兴趣，不关心</td><td>public apathy toward voting<br>公众对投票的冷漠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Widespread apathy among young voters worried the campaign organizers.  年轻选民中普遍的冷漠让竞选组织者担忧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 indifference</td><td>/ɪnˈdɪfərəns/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>漠不关心，冷淡</td><td>treat with indifference<br>以冷淡的态度对待</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crowd greeted the announcement with total indifference.  人群对这个通告完全漠不关心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 nonchalance</td><td>/ˌnɑːnʃəˈlɑːns/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>冷漠，漠不关心</td><td>studied nonchalance<br>刻意的淡然</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She accepted the important award with surprising nonchalance.  她以出人意料的淡然接受了这个重要奖项。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 torpor</td><td>/ˈtɔːrpər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>缺乏兴趣；（肉体或精神上的）迟钝，懒散</td><td>sink into torpor<br>陷入迟钝麻木</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The heat sank the whole village into a heavy afternoon torpor.  暑热让整个村子陷入沉沉的午后麻木。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unconcern</del></td><td>/ˌʌnkənˈsɜːrn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不关心，漠不关心</td><td>apparent unconcern for others<br>对他人明显的漠视</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His unconcern about the deadline annoyed the whole team.  他对截止日期的漠不关心让整个团队恼火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>zealot</b></td><td>/ˈzelət/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>狂热者，极端主义者</td><td>a religious zealot<br>宗教狂热者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only a zealot would defend that policy so fiercely.  只有狂热者才会如此激烈地为那项政策辩护。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enthusiast</td><td>/ɪnˈθuːziæst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>热衷者，爱好者</td><td>a sports enthusiast<br>体育爱好者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The club is full of cycling enthusiasts who ride every weekend.  俱乐部里全是每周末都骑车的自行车爱好者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 extremist</td><td>/ɪkˈstriːmɪst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>极端主义者；极端的</td><td>a religious extremist<br>宗教极端分子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The group expelled one extremist who openly advocated violence.  该团体开除了一名公开主张暴力的极端分子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fanatic</td><td>/fəˈnætɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>狂热者</td><td>a fitness fanatic<br>健身狂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My roommate is a fitness fanatic who trains twice a day.  我室友是个健身狂，一天训练两次。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fiend</td><td>/ˈfiːnd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>恶魔，残忍之人；狂热者</td><td>a cruel fiend<br>残忍的恶魔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The newspapers described the killer as a heartless fiend.  报纸把这个凶手描述成毫无心肠的恶魔。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 freak</td><td>/ˈfriːk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>狂热爱好者；怪人</td><td>a fitness freak<br>健身狂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My younger brother is a complete film freak.  我弟弟是个十足的电影狂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 partisan</td><td>/ˈpɑːrtəzn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>党派偏见的；盲目拥护者</td><td>partisan politics<br>党派偏见的政治</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The debate quickly sank into partisan shouting about very old grievances.  辩论很快沦为带党派偏见的叫嚷，纠缠陈年旧怨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 maniac</td><td>/ˈmeɪniæk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>狂热者；疯子</td><td>drive like a maniac<br>开车像疯子一样</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is a football maniac who never misses a single match.  他是个足球狂，一场比赛都不落。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nut</td><td>/ˈnʌt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>难题；坚果</td><td>a hard nut to crack<br>难解决的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Funding the repairs is a hard nut to crack.  筹措维修资金是个难解决的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 moderate</td><td>/ˈmɑːdəreɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>缓和，减轻；适度的</td><td>moderate one's demands<br>降低自己的要求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He agreed to moderate his tone during the debate.  他同意在辩论中缓和语气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>zenith</b></td><td>/ˈzenɪθ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>最高点，巅峰</td><td>at the zenith of<br>在…的巅峰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The empire stood at the zenith of its power around that year.  大约在那一年，这个帝国正处于国力的巅峰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 acme</td><td>/ˈækmi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>顶点，极点</td><td>the acme of perfection<br>完美的极致</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That performance marked the acme of her long career.  那场演出标志着她漫长职业生涯的顶峰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 apex</td><td>/ˈeɪˌpeks/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>顶点，最高点</td><td>the apex of his career<br>他事业的顶峰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At the apex of her fame she suddenly left the stage.  在名声的顶峰，她突然离开了舞台。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 apogee</td><td>/ˈæpəˌdʒiː/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>最高点，极点</td><td>the apogee of power<br>权力的顶点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The empire reached its apogee under this remarkably able emperor.  这个帝国在这位极有才干的皇帝治下达到极盛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 climax</td><td>/ˈklaɪˌmæks/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>高潮，顶点</td><td>the climax of the story<br>故事的高潮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film builds slowly toward a violent and unexpected climax.  影片缓慢推向一个暴烈而意外的高潮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crescendo</td><td>/krəˈʃendoʊ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（渐强之后到达的）顶峰</td><td>reach a crescendo<br>达到顶峰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Complaints about the choking smoke rose to a deafening crescendo.  对令人窒息的烟雾的抱怨达到了震耳欲聋的顶点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crest</td><td>/krest/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>顶部，浪尖，山顶</td><td>the crest of a wave<br>浪尖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The surfers waited patiently for the crest of the next big wave.  冲浪者们耐心等待下一个大浪的浪尖。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 peak</td><td>/ˈpiːk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>顶峰，最高点</td><td>at the peak of<br>在…的顶峰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sales peaked in December and then fell sharply in January.  销售在十二月达到顶峰，一月急剧下滑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pinnacle</td><td>/ˈpɪnəkl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>顶峰，巅峰</td><td>the pinnacle of success<br>成功的顶峰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At thirty she had reached the pinnacle of her profession.  三十岁时她已达到职业生涯的巅峰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 summit</td><td>/ˈsʌmɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>顶点</td><td>reach the summit<br>登上顶峰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The climbers reached the summit just before the storm closed in.  登山者在风暴逼近前登上顶峰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 top</td><td>/ˈtɑːp/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>顶点，最高处</td><td>at the top of the list<br>在名单最上方</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her name sat at the top of the shortlist.  她的名字排在候选名单最上方。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bottom</td><td>/ˈbɑːtəm/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>底部，最低点</td><td>at the bottom<br>在底部</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their team finished at the bottom of the league again.  他们的球队又一次排在联赛末位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 nadir</td><td>/ˈneɪdɪr/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>最低点，谷底</td><td>reach the nadir<br>到达最低点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His confidence reached its nadir after the third rejection.  第三次被拒后他的信心降到了最低点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>zesty</b></td><td>/ˈzesti/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>味浓的；有滋味的</td><td>a zesty sauce<br>味道浓烈的酱汁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A zesty tomato sauce made the plain noodles taste much better.  一份味浓的番茄酱让清淡的面条好吃了许多。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 peppery</td><td>/ˈpepəri/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>辛辣的；易怒的</td><td>a peppery stew<br>辛辣的炖菜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The peppery stew made everyone at the table reach for water.  辛辣的炖菜让桌上每个人都去拿水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 piquant</td><td>/ˈpiːkənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人振奋的</td><td>a piquant remark<br>令人振奋的妙语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His piquant remarks kept the whole table laughing through dinner.  他令人振奋的妙语让整桌人整顿晚餐都在笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pungent</td><td>/ˈpʌndʒənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（言辞）辛辣尖刻的；刺鼻的</td><td>pungent criticism<br>辛辣的批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His pungent criticism of the report left everyone at the table silent.  他对这份报告辛辣的批评让全桌人都沉默了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 salty</td><td>/ˈsɔːlti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>咸的；有滋味的</td><td>salty sea air<br>带咸味的海风</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The salty sea air stung our faces out on the pier.  带咸味的海风刺痛了我们在码头上的脸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 savory</td><td>/ˈseɪvəri/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>美味的，可口的</td><td>a savory dish<br>一道可口的菜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She served a savory pie made with mushrooms and herbs.  她端上一道用蘑菇和香草做的美味派。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spicy</td><td>/ˈspaɪsi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>辛辣的；刺激有趣的</td><td>spicy noodles<br>辣味面条</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We ordered spicy noodles and regretted it within a few minutes.  我们点了辣味面条，几分钟后就后悔了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 zingy</td><td>/ˈzɪŋi/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>刺激爽口的，活泼的</td><td>a zingy lemon dressing<br>爽口的柠檬调味汁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She added lime juice for a zingy finish to the salad.  她加了青柠汁，给沙拉带来爽口的收尾。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bland</td><td>/blænd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无趣的</td><td>a bland performance<br>平淡的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The critics called it a bland performance with no real feeling.  评论家称这是一场毫无真情、十分无趣的表演。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 insipid</td><td>/ɪnˈsɪpɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>平淡的，无聊的</td><td>an insipid conversation<br>无聊的谈话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film was so insipid that half the audience fell asleep.  这部电影平淡无聊，半数观众都睡着了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vapid</td><td>/ˈvæpɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无趣的，乏味的</td><td>a vapid conversation<br>乏味的谈话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The party ended in vapid conversation about traffic and the weather.  聚会最后只剩下关于交通和天气的乏味闲谈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 zestless</del></td><td>/ˈzestləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>乏味的，无生气的</td><td>a zestless performance<br>乏味的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The zestless performance left the audience checking their watches.  乏味的表演让观众不停看表。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>
